# ==============================================================================
# Configuração Isolada do Banco de Dados PostgreSQL (Pasta Database/)
# ==============================================================================
POSTGRES_USER=postgres
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=postgres
POSTGRES_DB=black_amber_db
POSTGRES_PORT=5432

# Porta do painel visual web (Adminer)
ADMINER_PORT=8080


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO [002/252]: Database/database.sql
SEÇÃO: 1. Banco de Dados (Database) | LINHAS: 260
========================================================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- Schema Mestre para PostgreSQL 16 — Black Amber Coffee Full-Stack
-- Idempotente (seguro para re-execução automática no boot do Backend / Render)
-- =============================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- Trigger utilitária para atualizar updated_at automaticamente
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.atualizar_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = TIMEZONE('utc', NOW());
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- =============================================================================
-- Tipos ENUM (criação idempotente)
-- =============================================================================

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.worker_roles AS ENUM ('barista', 'caixa', 'gerente', 'atendente');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.order_status AS ENUM ('criado', 'em_preparo', 'pronto', 'finalizado', 'cancelado');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.product_category AS ENUM ('cafe', 'cha', 'suco', 'smoothie', 'lanche', 'sobremesa', 'outro');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.payment_method AS ENUM ('pix', 'cartao_credito', 'cartao_debito', 'dinheiro');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.payment_status AS ENUM ('pendente', 'confirmado', 'recusado', 'estornado');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.attendance_status AS ENUM ('presente', 'falta', 'falta_justificada', 'folga');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

-- =============================================================================
-- Tabelas (3FN, PKs internas + public_id UUID, FKs com ON DELETE seguro)
-- =============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.workers (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  role public.worker_roles NOT NULL,
  salary NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_admin BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clients (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.password_reset_tokens (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  email TEXT NOT NULL,
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  used BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.products (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  category public.product_category NOT NULL,
  price NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (price > 0),
  img_url TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stocks (
  product_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  min_quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.inventory_items (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT UNIQUE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  category TEXT,
  quantity NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  unit TEXT NOT NULL,
  min_quantity NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  img_url TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.carts (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES public.clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.cart_items (
  cart_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.carts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  product_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
  observation TEXT,
  added_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  PRIMARY KEY (cart_id, product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.orders (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  client_id INTEGER REFERENCES public.clients(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  status public.order_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'criado',
  total_amount NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  observation TEXT,
  updated_by INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.order_items (
  order_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  product_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
  unit_price NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
  observation TEXT,
  PRIMARY KEY (order_id, product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.payments (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  amount NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
  method public.payment_method NOT NULL,
  status public.payment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pendente',
  paid_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.order_status_history (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  previous_status public.order_status NOT NULL,
  new_status public.order_status NOT NULL,
  changed_by_worker INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.attendance (
  worker_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  work_date DATE NOT NULL,
  status public.attendance_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'presente',
  notes TEXT,
  recorded_by INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  PRIMARY KEY (worker_id, work_date)
);

-- Índices para Performance de Consultas
-- =============================================================================

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_is_active ON public.workers (is_active);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clients_is_active ON public.clients (is_active);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_category ON public.products (category);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_is_active ON public.products (is_active);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_status ON public.orders (status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_client_id ON public.orders (client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_created_at ON public.orders (created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_password_reset_email ON public.password_reset_tokens (email);

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_workers_updated_at ON public.workers;

ARQUIVO [003/252]: Database/docker-compose.yml
SEÇÃO: 1. Banco de Dados (Database) | LINHAS: 51

services:
  postgres:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: black-amber-postgres
    restart: unless-stopped
    env_file:
      - path: ./.env
        required: false
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-postgres}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-postgres}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-black_amber_db}
    ports:
      - "${POSTGRES_PORT:-5432}:5432"
    volumes:
      - black_amber_postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    networks:
      - black-amber-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${POSTGRES_USER:-postgres} -d ${POSTGRES_DB:-black_amber_db}"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5
      start_period: 5s

  adminer:
    image: adminer:latest
    container_name: black-amber-db-adminer
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "${ADMINER_PORT:-8080}:8080"
    environment:
      ADMINER_DEFAULT_SERVER: postgres
      ADMINER_DESIGN: pepa-linha-dark
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    networks:
      - black-amber-network

volumes:
  black_amber_postgres_data:
    name: black-amber-postgres-data

networks:
  black-amber-network:
    driver: bridge
    name: black-amber-network

FROM postgres:16-alpine

# Executa automaticamente o schema mestre e a seed inicial na inicialização do container
COPY ./database.sql /docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/01-database.sql
COPY ./seeds/01_seed_black_amber.sql /docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/02-seed.sql

EXPOSE 5432

ARQUIVO [005/252]: Database/migrations/001_initial_schema.sql

-- =============================================================================
-- Schema Mestre para PostgreSQL 16 — Black Amber Coffee Full-Stack
-- Idempotente (seguro para re-execução automática no boot do Backend / Render)
-- =============================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- Trigger utilitária para atualizar updated_at automaticamente
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.atualizar_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = TIMEZONE('utc', NOW());
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- =============================================================================
-- Tipos ENUM (criação idempotente)
-- =============================================================================

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.worker_roles AS ENUM ('barista', 'caixa', 'gerente', 'atendente');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.order_status AS ENUM ('criado', 'em_preparo', 'pronto', 'finalizado', 'cancelado');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.product_category AS ENUM ('cafe', 'cha', 'suco', 'smoothie', 'lanche', 'sobremesa', 'outro');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.payment_method AS ENUM ('pix', 'cartao_credito', 'cartao_debito', 'dinheiro');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.payment_status AS ENUM ('pendente', 'confirmado', 'recusado', 'estornado');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE public.attendance_status AS ENUM ('presente', 'falta', 'falta_justificada', 'folga');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

-- =============================================================================
-- Tabelas (3FN, PKs internas + public_id UUID, FKs com ON DELETE seguro)
-- =============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.workers (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  role public.worker_roles NOT NULL,
  salary NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_admin BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clients (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.password_reset_tokens (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  email TEXT NOT NULL,
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,

  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

  img_url TEXT,

  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.carts (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES public.clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.cart_items (
  cart_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.carts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  product_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
  observation TEXT,
  added_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  PRIMARY KEY (cart_id, product_id)

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.orders (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,

  observation TEXT,
  updated_by INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,

  PRIMARY KEY (order_id, product_id)

);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.order_status_history (

ARQUIVO [007/252]: Database/seeds/01_seed_black_amber.sql
SEÇÃO: 1. Banco de Dados (Database) | LINHAS: 60

-- =============================================================================
-- Seed Inicial Determinística — Black Amber Coffee
-- Idempotente: utiliza ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING / verificações WHERE NOT EXISTS
-- Senha padrão das contas de demonstração: 123456
-- =============================================================================

-- 1. Funcionários (Workers)
INSERT INTO public.workers (email, password, full_name, phone, role, salary, is_admin, is_active)
VALUES
  ('admin@blackamber.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'Gerente Black Amber', '11999999999', 'gerente', 5500.00, TRUE, TRUE),
  ('barista@blackamber.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'João Barista', '11988888888', 'barista', 2800.00, FALSE, TRUE),
  ('caixa@blackamber.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'Carlos Caixa', '11977777777', 'caixa', 2500.00, FALSE, TRUE),
  ('atendente@blackamber.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'Maria Atendente', '11966666666', 'atendente', 2400.00, FALSE, TRUE)
ON CONFLICT (email) DO NOTHING;

-- 2. Clientes
INSERT INTO public.clients (email, password, full_name, phone, is_active)
VALUES
  ('cliente@teste.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'Cliente Teste', '11955555555', TRUE),
  ('maria@email.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'Maria Silva', '11911111111', TRUE),
  ('joao@email.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'João Pereira', '11922222222', TRUE),
  ('ana@email.com', crypt('123456', gen_salt('bf', 10)), 'Ana Costa', '11933333333', TRUE)
ON CONFLICT (email) DO NOTHING;

-- 3. Produtos e Estoques
INSERT INTO public.products (name, description, category, price, is_active)
SELECT v.name, v.description, v.category::public.product_category, v.price, TRUE
FROM (
  VALUES
    ('Café Expresso', 'Café puro e encorpado', 'cafe', 5.00),
    ('Café Latte', 'Café com leite vaporizado', 'cafe', 8.00),
    ('Cappuccino', 'Café com leite e espuma cremosa', 'cafe', 10.00),
    ('Mocha', 'Café com chocolate e leite', 'cafe', 12.00),
    ('Suco de Laranja', 'Suco natural de laranja', 'suco', 7.00),
    ('Água Mineral', 'Água sem gás 500ml', 'outro', 3.00),
    ('Croissant', 'Croissant artesanal de manteiga', 'lanche', 6.00),
    ('Bolo de Cenoura', 'Fatia de bolo de cenoura com cobertura de chocolate', 'sobremesa', 8.00),
    ('Sanduíche Natural', 'Sanduíche integral com frango e salada', 'lanche', 15.00)
) AS v(name, description, category, price)
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
  SELECT 1 FROM public.products p WHERE p.name = v.name
);

INSERT INTO public.stocks (product_id, quantity, min_quantity)
SELECT p.id, 30, 5
FROM public.products p
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
  SELECT 1 FROM public.stocks s WHERE s.product_id = p.id
);

-- 4. Itens de Inventário (Insumos)
INSERT INTO public.inventory_items (code, name, description, category, quantity, unit, min_quantity)
VALUES

ARQUIVO [008/252]: Backend/core/config/env.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 83

import "dotenv/config";
import { z } from "zod";

const isTestEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV === "test";

const envSchema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV: z.enum(["development", "production", "test"]).default("development"),
  PORT: z.coerce.number().default(3001),
  PUBLIC_PORT: z.coerce.number().optional(),
  CORS_ORIGIN: z.string().default("*"),

  DATABASE_URL: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  DB_HOST: z.string().default("localhost"),
  DB_PORT: z.coerce.number().default(5432),
  DB_USER: z.string().default("postgres"),
  DB_PASSWORD: z.string().default("postgres"),
  DB_NAME: z.string().default("black_amber_db"),
  DB_SSL: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((v) => v === "true"),
  AUTO_SEED: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((v) => v !== "false"),

  JWT_SECRET: z
    .string()
    .min(16, "JWT_SECRET deve ter pelo menos 16 caracteres.")
    .default(isTestEnv ? "test_jwt_secret_at_least_16chars" : "black_amber_fallback_jwt_secret_16"),
  JWT_EXPIRATION: z.string().default("15m"),
  JWT_SECRET_REFRESH: z.string().min(16).optional(),
  REFRESH_SECRET: z.string().min(16).optional(),

  BUCKET_NAME: z.string().default(isTestEnv ? "test-bucket" : ""),
  BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY: z.string().default(isTestEnv ? "test-access-key" : ""),
  BUCKET_SECRET_KEY: z.string().default(isTestEnv ? "test-secret-key" : ""),
  BUCKET_URL: z.string().default(isTestEnv ? "http://localhost:9000" : ""),
  BUCKET_PUBLIC_URL: z.string().default(""),
  BUCKET_REGION: z.string().default("us-east-1"),

  RESEND_API_KEY: z.string().default(isTestEnv ? "re_test_key" : ""),
  SYSTEM_MAIL_ADDR: z.string().default(isTestEnv ? "test@blackamber.com" : "onboarding@resend.dev"),
});

export type EnvConfig = z.infer<typeof envSchema> & {
  DATABASE_URL: string;
  isDev: boolean;
  isTest: boolean;
  corsOrigins: string[] | "*";
};

function validateEnv(): EnvConfig {
  const parsed = envSchema.safeParse(process.env);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    console.error("[Env] Variáveis de ambiente inválidas:", parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors);
    throw new Error("Variáveis de ambiente inválidas. Verifique a configuração do servidor.");
  }

  const data = parsed.data;
  const databaseUrl =
    data.DATABASE_URL ||
    `postgresql://${encodeURIComponent(data.DB_USER)}:${encodeURIComponent(data.DB_PASSWORD)}@${data.DB_HOST}:${data.DB_PORT}/${data.DB_NAME}`;

  const corsOrigins =
    data.CORS_ORIGIN.trim() === "*" || data.CORS_ORIGIN.trim() === ""
      ? "*"
      : data.CORS_ORIGIN.split(",")
          .map((origin) => origin.trim())
          .filter(Boolean);

  return {
    ...data,
    DATABASE_URL: databaseUrl,
    isDev: data.NODE_ENV === "development",
    isTest: data.NODE_ENV === "test",
    corsOrigins,
  };
}

export const env = validateEnv();

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { pool } from "./postgres.client.js";
import { logger } from "../errors/index.js";

const currentDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

const EMBEDDED_SCHEMA_SQL = `
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.atualizar_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = TIMEZONE('utc', NOW());
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE public.worker_roles AS ENUM ('barista', 'caixa', 'gerente', 'atendente'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;
DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE public.order_status AS ENUM ('criado', 'em_preparo', 'pronto', 'finalizado', 'cancelado'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;
DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE public.product_category AS ENUM ('cafe', 'cha', 'suco', 'smoothie', 'lanche', 'sobremesa', 'outro'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;
DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE public.payment_method AS ENUM ('pix', 'cartao_credito', 'cartao_debito', 'dinheiro'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;
DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE public.payment_status AS ENUM ('pendente', 'confirmado', 'recusado', 'estornado'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;
DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE public.attendance_status AS ENUM ('presente', 'falta', 'falta_justificada', 'folga'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.workers (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  role public.worker_roles NOT NULL,
  salary NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_admin BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clients (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.password_reset_tokens (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  email TEXT NOT NULL,
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  used BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.products (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  category public.product_category NOT NULL,
  price NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (price > 0),
  img_url TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stocks (
  product_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  min_quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.inventory_items (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT UNIQUE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  category TEXT,
  quantity NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  unit TEXT NOT NULL,
  min_quantity NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  img_url TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.carts (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES public.clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.cart_items (
  cart_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.carts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  product_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
  observation TEXT,
  added_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  PRIMARY KEY (cart_id, product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.orders (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  public_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  client_id INTEGER REFERENCES public.clients(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  status public.order_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'criado',
  total_amount NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  observation TEXT,
  updated_by INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.order_items (
  order_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  product_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.products(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
  unit_price NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
  observation TEXT,
  PRIMARY KEY (order_id, product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.payments (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  amount NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
  method public.payment_method NOT NULL,
  status public.payment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pendente',
  paid_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.order_status_history (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  previous_status public.order_status NOT NULL,
  new_status public.order_status NOT NULL,
  changed_by_worker INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW())
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.attendance (
  worker_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  work_date DATE NOT NULL,
  status public.attendance_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'presente',
  notes TEXT,
  recorded_by INTEGER REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc', NOW()),
  PRIMARY KEY (worker_id, work_date)
);
`;

function resolveDatabaseSql(): string {
  const candidates = [
    path.resolve(process.cwd(), "..", "Database", "database.sql"),
    path.resolve(currentDir, "..", "..", "..", "Database", "database.sql"),
    path.resolve(currentDir, "..", "..", "..", "..", "Database", "database.sql"),
  ];

  for (const filePath of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      return fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
    }
  }

  return EMBEDDED_SCHEMA_SQL;
}

/**
 * Ensures all PostgreSQL schemas, ENUM types, indexes, and triggers exist on startup.
 */
export async function runMigrations(): Promise<void> {
  const sql = resolveDatabaseSql();
  await pool.query(sql);
  logger.info("[Database] Schema PostgreSQL verificado/aplicado com sucesso.");
}

ARQUIVO [010/252]: Backend/core/database/postgres.client.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 67
========================================================================================
import { Pool, type PoolClient, type QueryResult, type QueryResultRow } from "pg";
import { env } from "../config/env.js";

const isRenderOrRemoteSsl =
  env.DB_SSL ||
  env.DATABASE_URL.includes("render.com") ||
  env.DATABASE_URL.includes("sslmode=require");

export const pool = new Pool({
  connectionString: env.DATABASE_URL,
  ssl: isRenderOrRemoteSsl ? { rejectUnauthorized: false } : false,
  max: 20,
  idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
  connectionTimeoutMillis: 10_000,
});

pool.on("error", (err) => {
  console.error("[PostgreSQL Pool] Erro inesperado em cliente ocioso:", err);
});

/**
 * Executes a parameterized SQL query against the shared PostgreSQL connection pool.
 */
export async function query<T extends QueryResultRow = QueryResultRow>(
  text: string,
  params: readonly unknown[] = [],
): Promise<QueryResult<T>> {
  return pool.query<T>(text, [...params]);
}

/**
 * Runs an operation inside an explicit ACID database transaction (`BEGIN` / `COMMIT` / `ROLLBACK`).
 */
export async function withTransaction<T>(fn: (client: PoolClient) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");
    const result = await fn(client);
    await client.query("COMMIT");
    return result;
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw error;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
}

/**
 * Verifies live database connectivity via `SELECT 1` and measures query latency in milliseconds.
 */
export async function checkDatabaseHealth(): Promise<{ connected: boolean; latencyMs: number }> {
  const start = Date.now();
  try {
    await pool.query("SELECT 1");
    return {
      connected: true,
      latencyMs: Date.now() - start,
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      connected: false,
      latencyMs: Date.now() - start,
    };
  }
}

import { pool, withTransaction } from "./postgres.client.js";

const INVENTORY_SEEDS: ReadonlyArray<{
  code: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  category: string;
  quantity: number;
  unit: string;
  minQuantity: number;
}> = [
  { code: "INV-001", name: "Grãos de Café Arábica", description: "Torra média especial para espresso", category: "Matéria-Prima", quantity: 25, unit: "kg", minQuantity: 10 },

/**
 * Populates initial workers, clients, products, stocks, inventory items, and demo orders idempotently.
 */
export async function runSeed(): Promise<void> {
  const hashedPassword = await SecurityUtils.hashPassword("123456");

  for (const w of WORKER_SEEDS) {
    await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO public.workers (email, password, full_name, phone, role, salary, is_admin, is_active)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::public.worker_roles, $6, $7, TRUE)
       ON CONFLICT (email) DO NOTHING`,
      [w.email, hashedPassword, w.fullName, w.phone, w.role, w.salary, w.role === "gerente"],
    );
  }

  for (const c of CLIENT_SEEDS) {
    await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO public.clients (email, password, full_name, phone, is_active)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, TRUE)
       ON CONFLICT (email) DO NOTHING`,
      [c.email, hashedPassword, c.fullName, c.phone],
    );
  }

  for (const p of PRODUCT_SEEDS) {
    const existing = await pool.query<{ id: number }>(
      "SELECT id FROM public.products WHERE name = $1 LIMIT 1",
      [p.name],
    );

    let productId: number;
    if (existing.rows.length === 0) {
      const inserted = await pool.query<{ id: number }>(
        `INSERT INTO public.products (name, description, price, category, is_active)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4::public.product_category, TRUE)
         RETURNING id`,
        [p.name, p.description, p.price, p.category],
      );
      productId = inserted.rows[0].id;
    } else {
      productId = existing.rows[0].id;
    }

    await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO public.stocks (product_id, quantity, min_quantity)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
       ON CONFLICT (product_id) DO NOTHING`,
      [productId, p.quantity, p.minQuantity],
    );
  }

  for (const inv of INVENTORY_SEEDS) {
    await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO public.inventory_items (code, name, description, category, quantity, unit, min_quantity)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)
       ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING`,
      [inv.code, inv.name, inv.description, inv.category, inv.quantity, inv.unit, inv.minQuantity],
    );
  }

  const orderCountResult = await pool.query<{ total: string }>("SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS total FROM public.orders");
  const existingOrdersCount = Number(orderCountResult.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);

  if (existingOrdersCount === 0) {
    const clientsRes = await pool.query<{ id: number; email: string }>("SELECT id, email FROM public.clients");
    const productsRes = await pool.query<{ id: number; name: string; price: string }>(
      "SELECT id, name, price FROM public.products",
    );
    const workersRes = await pool.query<{ id: number }>("SELECT id FROM public.workers LIMIT 1");

    const clientTest = clientsRes.rows.find((c) => c.email === "cliente@teste.com") ?? clientsRes.rows[0];
    const otherClients = clientsRes.rows.filter((c) => c.id !== clientTest?.id);
    const firstWorkerId = workersRes.rows[0]?.id ?? null;

    if (clientTest && productsRes.rows.length > 0) {
      const findProduct = (name: string) =>
        productsRes.rows.find((p) => p.name === name) ?? productsRes.rows[0];

      const now = new Date();
      const daysAgo = (days: number) => new Date(now.getTime() - days * 86_400_000);

      const sampleOrders: Array<{
        status: OrderStatusType;
        paymentMethod: PaymentMethodType | null;
        observation: string | null;
        clientId: number;
        createdAt: Date;
        items: Array<{ name: string; quantity: number }>;
      }> = [
        {
          status: OrderStatus.CRIADO,
          paymentMethod: "pix",
          observation: "Cliente solicita canela extra",
          clientId: clientTest.id,
          createdAt: now,
          items: [
            { name: "Café Latte", quantity: 2 },
            { name: "Croissant", quantity: 1 },
          ],
        },
        {
          status: OrderStatus.EM_PREPARO,
          paymentMethod: "cartao_credito",
          observation: null,
          clientId: clientTest.id,
          createdAt: now,
          items: [{ name: "Cappuccino", quantity: 1 }],
        },
        {
          status: OrderStatus.PRONTO,
          paymentMethod: "pix",
          observation: "Pedido pronto - cliente aguardando",
          clientId: otherClients[0]?.id ?? clientTest.id,
          createdAt: now,
          items: [
            { name: "Sanduíche Natural", quantity: 1 },
            { name: "Suco de Laranja", quantity: 1 },
          ],
        },
        {
          status: OrderStatus.FINALIZADO,
          paymentMethod: "dinheiro",
          observation: "Mesa 5",
          clientId: otherClients[1]?.id ?? clientTest.id,
          createdAt: daysAgo(1),
          items: [
            { name: "Café Expresso", quantity: 3 },
            { name: "Bolo de Cenoura", quantity: 2 },
          ],
        },
        {
          status: OrderStatus.CANCELADO,
          paymentMethod: null,
          observation: "Cliente desistiu",
          clientId: clientTest.id,
          createdAt: daysAgo(2),
          items: [{ name: "Mocha", quantity: 1 }],
        },
      ];

      for (const orderData of sampleOrders) {
        await withTransaction(async (client) => {
          const orderItems = orderData.items.map((it) => {
            const prod = findProduct(it.name);
            return {
              productId: prod.id,
              quantity: it.quantity,
              unitPrice: Number(prod.price),
            };
          });

          const totalAmount = orderItems.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.unitPrice * i.quantity, 0);
          const code = SecurityUtils.generateOrderCode();

          const insertedOrder = await client.query<{ id: number }>(
            `INSERT INTO public.orders (code, client_id, status, total_amount, observation, created_at, updated_at)
             VALUES ($1, $2, $3::public.order_status, $4, $5, $6, $6)
             RETURNING id`,
            [code, orderData.clientId, orderData.status, totalAmount.toFixed(2), orderData.observation, orderData.createdAt],
          );

          const orderId = insertedOrder.rows[0].id;

          for (const item of orderItems) {
            await client.query(
              `INSERT INTO public.order_items (order_id, product_id, quantity, unit_price)
               VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)`,
              [orderId, item.productId, item.quantity, item.unitPrice.toFixed(2)],
            );
          }

          if (orderData.paymentMethod) {
            const paymentStatus = orderData.status === OrderStatus.FINALIZADO ? "confirmado" : "pendente";
            await client.query(
              `INSERT INTO public.payments (order_id, amount, method, status, paid_at, created_at, updated_at)
               VALUES ($1, $2, $3::public.payment_method, $4::public.payment_status, $5, $6, $6)`,
              [
                orderId,
                totalAmount.toFixed(2),
                orderData.paymentMethod,
                paymentStatus,
                paymentStatus === "confirmado" ? orderData.createdAt : null,
                orderData.createdAt,
              ],
            );
          }

          await client.query(
            `INSERT INTO public.order_status_history (order_id, previous_status, new_status, changed_by_worker, changed_at)
             VALUES ($1, $2::public.order_status, $3::public.order_status, $4, $5)`,
            [orderId, orderData.status, orderData.status, firstWorkerId, orderData.createdAt],
          );
        });
      }
    }
  }

  logger.info("[Seed] Dados iniciais verificados com sucesso.");
}

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import pino from "pino";
import { ZodError } from "zod";
import { env } from "../config/env.js";

export const logger = pino({
  level: env.isTest ? "silent" : env.isDev ? "debug" : "info",
});

export class AppError extends Error {
  public readonly statusCode: number;
  public readonly code: string;

  constructor(code: string, statusCode?: number, message?: string) {
    super(code);
    this.code = code;
    this.statusCode = statusCode ?? errorMap[code]?.status ?? 500;
    if (message) {
      this.name = message;
    }
  }
}

export const errorMap: Record<string, { status: number; message: string }> = {
  INVALID_CREDENTIALS: { status: 401, message: "Email ou senha inválidos." },
  INVALID_TOKEN: { status: 401, message: "Token inválido ou expirado." },

  EMAIL_ALREADY_IN_USE: { status: 409, message: "Este email já está registrado." },
  CLIENT_ALREADY_EXISTS: { status: 409, message: "Já existe um cliente com esse email." },
  CLIENT_NOT_FOUND: { status: 404, message: "Cliente não encontrado." },
  USER_NOT_FOUND: { status: 404, message: "Usuário não encontrado." },

  PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND: { status: 404, message: "Produto não encontrado." },
  PRODUCT_INACTIVE: { status: 400, message: "Produto inativo." },
  INVENTORY_NOT_FOUND: { status: 404, message: "Item de inventário não encontrado." },
  CART_EMPTY: { status: 400, message: "O carrinho está vazio." },

  UPDATE_REQUIRES_FIELDS: { status: 400, message: "Pelo menos um campo deve ser fornecido para atualização." },
  INVALID_FILE_TYPE: { status: 400, message: "Arquivo inválido. Envie uma imagem JPEG, PNG ou WebP." },
  LIMIT_FILE_SIZE: { status: 400, message: "Arquivo muito grande. O limite é 5MB." },
  STORAGE_NOT_CONFIGURED: {
    status: 503,
    message: "Armazenamento de imagens (S3/R2) não configurado nas variáveis de ambiente.",
  },
  MAIL_NOT_CONFIGURED: {
    status: 503,
    message: "Serviço de e-mail (Resend) não configurado nas variáveis de ambiente.",
  },
  NOT_FOUND: { status: 404, message: "Recurso não encontrado." },
  BAD_REQUEST: { status: 400, message: "Solicitação inválida." },
  DATABASE_CONNECTION_ERROR: { status: 500, message: "Erro de conexão com o banco de dados." },
  INTERNAL_ERROR: { status: 500, message: "Erro interno do servidor. Tente novamente mais tarde." },
};

export function globalErrorHandler(
  err: unknown,
  _req: Request,
  res: Response,
  _next: NextFunction,
): void {
  if (err instanceof ZodError) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: {
        code: "BAD_REQUEST",
        message: err.issues[0]?.message ?? "Dados inválidos na requisição.",
        details: err.issues,
      },
    });
    return;
  }

  let code = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "INTERNAL_ERROR";

  if (err instanceof AppError) {
    code = err.code;
  }

  const mapped = errorMap[code] ?? {
    status: 500,
    message: "Erro interno no servidor.",
  };

  if (mapped.status === 500) {
    logger.error({ err }, "Erro interno não mapeado");
    code = "INTERNAL_ERROR";
  }

  res.status(mapped.status).json({
    error: {
      code,
      message: env.isDev && mapped.status === 500 && err instanceof Error ? err.message : mapped.message,
    },
  });
}

ARQUIVO [014/252]: Backend/core/jwt/jwt.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 80

import jwt, { type SignOptions } from "jsonwebtoken";
import { env } from "../config/env.js";

export interface JWTPayload {
  id: number;
  email: string;
  publicId: string;
  role?: string;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
}

export interface PasswordResetPayload {
  userId: number;
  publicUserId: string;
}

export class JWTService {
  private readonly secret: string;
  private readonly expiration: SignOptions["expiresIn"];
  private readonly refreshSecret: string;

  constructor() {
    this.secret = env.JWT_SECRET;
    this.expiration = this.normalizeExpiration(env.JWT_EXPIRATION);
    this.refreshSecret = env.JWT_SECRET_REFRESH ?? env.REFRESH_SECRET ?? env.JWT_SECRET;
  }

  private normalizeExpiration(value: string | undefined): SignOptions["expiresIn"] {
    if (!value) return "15m";
    const cleaned = value
      .split("///")[0]
      .trim()
      .replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, "");
    return (cleaned || "15m") as SignOptions["expiresIn"];
  }

  generateToken(payload: JWTPayload): string {
    return jwt.sign(payload, this.secret, { expiresIn: this.expiration });
  }

  verifyToken(token: string): JWTPayload | null {
    try {
      return jwt.verify(token, this.secret) as JWTPayload;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  extractTokenFromHeader(authHeader: string | undefined): string | null {
    if (!authHeader) return null;
    const parts = authHeader.split(" ");
    if (parts.length !== 2 || parts[0] !== "Bearer") return null;
    return parts[1];
  }

  generateRefreshToken(publicId: string): string {
    return jwt.sign({ id: publicId }, this.refreshSecret, { expiresIn: "30d" });
  }

  verifyRefreshToken(token: string): { id: string } | null {
    try {
      return jwt.verify(token, this.refreshSecret) as { id: string };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  generatePasswordResetToken(payload: PasswordResetPayload): string {
    return jwt.sign(payload, this.secret, { expiresIn: "10m" });
  }

  verifyPasswordResetToken(token: string): PasswordResetPayload | null {
    try {
      return jwt.verify(token, this.secret) as PasswordResetPayload;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

ARQUIVO [016/252]: Backend/core/mail/mail.provider.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 28

import { Resend } from "resend";
import type { IMailProvider, MailPayload } from "./mail.interface.js";
import { env } from "../config/env.js";

export class ResendProvider implements IMailProvider {
  private client: Resend | null = null;

  private getClient(): Resend {
    if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY) {
      throw new Error("MAIL_NOT_CONFIGURED");
    }
    if (!this.client) {
      this.client = new Resend(env.RESEND_API_KEY);
    }
    return this.client;
  }

  async send(options: MailPayload): Promise<void> {
    const client = this.getClient();
    await client.emails.send({
      from: env.SYSTEM_MAIL_ADDR,
      to: options.to,
      subject: options.subject,
      html: options.html,
    });
  }
}

ARQUIVO [017/252]: Backend/core/mail/mail.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 46

import React from "react";
import { render } from "@react-email/render";
import type { IMailProvider } from "./mail.interface.js";
import { ResendProvider } from "./mail.provider.js";
import { PasswordReset, type PasswordResetProps } from "./templates/reset-password.js";

export interface MailSchemaMap {
  PASSWORD_RESET: PasswordResetProps;
}

export type MailSchemaKey = keyof MailSchemaMap;

interface SchemaEntry<P> {
  subject: string;
  component: React.FC<P>;
}

const mailTemplates: { [K in MailSchemaKey]: SchemaEntry<MailSchemaMap[K]> } = {
  PASSWORD_RESET: {
    subject: "Redefinição de senha — Black Amber Coffee",
    component: PasswordReset,
  },
};

export interface SendMailOptions<S extends MailSchemaKey> {
  schema: S;
  to: string;
  data: MailSchemaMap[S];
}

export class MailService {
  constructor(private readonly provider: IMailProvider) {}

  async send<S extends MailSchemaKey>(options: SendMailOptions<S>): Promise<void> {
    const entry = mailTemplates[options.schema];
    const html = await render(React.createElement(entry.component, options.data));
    await this.provider.send({
      to: options.to,
      subject: entry.subject,
      html,
    });
  }
}

export const mailService = new MailService(new ResendProvider());

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { JWTService } from "../jwt/jwt.service.js";
import { pool } from "../database/postgres.client.js";

declare global {
  namespace Express {
    interface Request {
      user?: {
        id: number;
        email: string;
        publicId: string;
        role?: string;
        isAdmin?: boolean;
      };
    }
  }
}

const jwtService = new JWTService();

/**
 * Validates the Bearer JWT token and verifies that the account remains active in PostgreSQL.
 */
export async function AuthMiddleware(
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
    const token = jwtService.extractTokenFromHeader(authHeader);

    if (!token) {
      res.status(401).json({
        error: { code: "UNAUTHORIZED", message: "Token de autenticação não fornecido." },
      });
      return;
    }

    const payload = jwtService.verifyToken(token);

    if (!payload) {
      res.status(401).json({
        error: { code: "INVALID_TOKEN", message: "Token expirado ou inválido." },
      });
      return;
    }

    const isWorker = payload.role !== undefined || payload.isAdmin === true;
    const table = isWorker ? "public.workers" : "public.clients";

    const statusResult = await pool.query<{ is_active: boolean }>(
      `SELECT is_active FROM ${table} WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [payload.publicId],
    );

    if (statusResult.rows.length === 0) {
      res.status(401).json({
        error: { code: "UNAUTHORIZED", message: "Conta não encontrada." },
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!statusResult.rows[0].is_active) {
      res.status(403).json({
        error: {
          code: "ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED",
          message: "Esta conta está desativada. Contate o administrador.",
        },
      });
      return;
    }

    req.user = payload;
    next();
  } catch {
    res.status(401).json({
      error: { code: "UNAUTHORIZED", message: "Falha ao autenticar token." },
    });
  }
}

ARQUIVO [020/252]: Backend/core/middlewares/permission.middleware.ts

========================================================================================
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";

type UserRole = "admin" | "worker" | "user";

export function requireRole(...roles: UserRole[]) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    if (!req.user) {
      res.status(401).json({
        error: {
          code: "UNAUTHORIZED",
          message: "Token de autenticação não fornecido.",
        },
      });
      return;
    }

    const userRole: UserRole = req.user.isAdmin
      ? "admin"
      : req.user.role
        ? "worker"
        : "user";

    if (!roles.includes(userRole)) {
      res.status(403).json({
        error: {
          code: "FORBIDDEN",
          message: "Acesso negado. Você não tem permissão para esta operação.",
        },
      });
      return;
    }

    next();
  };
}

export function requireAdmin() {
  return requireRole("admin");
}

export function requireWorker() {
  return requireRole("worker", "admin");
}

ARQUIVO [021/252]: Backend/core/middlewares/security.middleware.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 124
========================================================================================
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";

const rateLimitStore = new Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }>();
const AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX = 25;
const API_RATE_LIMIT_MAX = 300;
const RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

const SENSITIVE_FIELDS = new Set([
  "password",
  "newPassword",
  "confirmNewPassword",
  "token",
  "refreshToken",
  "accessToken",
  "code",
]);

function maskSensitiveFields(obj: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(obj)) {
    result[key] = SENSITIVE_FIELDS.has(key) ? "***REDACTED***" : obj[key];
  }
  return result;
}

export function secureHeaders(_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  res.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
  res.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
  res.setHeader("X-XSS-Protection", "1; mode=block");
  res.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
  res.setHeader("Permissions-Policy", "geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=()");
  res.removeHeader("X-Powered-By");
  next();
}

export function rateLimit(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  if (req.path.endsWith("/health") || req.path === "/") {
    next();
    return;
  }

  const forwarded = req.headers["x-forwarded-for"];
  const ip =
    (typeof forwarded === "string" ? forwarded.split(",")[0]?.trim() : undefined) ||
    req.socket.remoteAddress ||
    "unknown";

  const isAuthRoute = req.path.includes("/auth/");
  const bucketKey = `${ip}:${isAuthRoute ? "auth" : "api"}`;
  const maxRequests = isAuthRoute ? AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX : API_RATE_LIMIT_MAX;
  const now = Date.now();

  const entry = rateLimitStore.get(bucketKey);

  if (!entry || now > entry.resetAt) {
    rateLimitStore.set(bucketKey, { count: 1, resetAt: now + RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS });
    next();
    return;
  }

  entry.count += 1;

  if (entry.count > maxRequests) {
    res.status(429).json({
      error: {
        code: "TOO_MANY_REQUESTS",
        message: "Muitas requisições. Tente novamente em 15 minutos.",
      },
    });
    return;
  }

  next();
}

export function bodySizeLimit(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  const contentType = req.headers["content-type"] ?? "";
  if (contentType.startsWith("multipart/form-data")) {
    next();
    return;
  }

  const MAX_BYTES = 1_048_576;
  const contentLength = parseInt(req.headers["content-length"] || "0", 10);

  if (contentLength > MAX_BYTES) {
    res.status(413).json({
      error: {
        code: "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE",
        message: "Corpo da requisição excede o limite de 1MB.",
      },
    });
    return;
  }

  next();
}

export function securityLogger(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  const sensitiveRoutes = ["/login", "/register", "/refresh", "/forgotpassword"];
  const isSensitive = sensitiveRoutes.some((r) => req.path.includes(r));

  if (isSensitive) {
    const safeBody =
      req.body && typeof req.body === "object" && !Array.isArray(req.body)
        ? maskSensitiveFields(req.body as Record<string, unknown>)
        : {};

    console.log(
      `[SECURITY] ${new Date().toISOString()} | ${req.method} ${req.originalUrl} | body: ${JSON.stringify(safeBody)}`,
    );

    res.on("finish", () => {
      if (res.statusCode === 401 || res.statusCode === 403) {
        console.warn(
          `[SECURITY ALERT] Failed auth attempt | ${req.method} ${req.originalUrl} | status: ${res.statusCode}`,
        );
      }
    });
  }

  next();
}

ARQUIVO [022/252]: Backend/core/middlewares/upload.middleware.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 55

import multer from "multer";
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { errorMap } from "../errors/index.js";

const storage = multer.memoryStorage();

export const avatarUpload = multer({
  storage,
  limits: {
    fileSize: 5 * 1024 * 1024,
  },
  fileFilter: (_req, file, cb) => {
    const allowedMimeTypes = new Set(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"]);

    if (!allowedMimeTypes.has(file.mimetype)) {
      cb(new Error("INVALID_FILE_TYPE"));
      return;
    }

    cb(null, true);
  },
});

export function avatarUploadMiddleware(
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction,
): void {
  avatarUpload.single("avatar")(req, res, (err) => {
    if (!err) {
      next();
      return;
    }

    const code =
      err instanceof multer.MulterError
        ? err.code
        : err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "INVALID_REQUEST";

    const mapped = errorMap[code] ?? {
      status: 400,
      message: "Falha ao processar upload da imagem.",
    };

    res.status(mapped.status).json({
      error: {
        code: mapped.status === 500 ? "INTERNAL_ERROR" : code,
        message: mapped.message,
      },
    });
  });
}

ARQUIVO [023/252]: Backend/core/middlewares/validation.middleware.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 33
========================================================================================
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { type ZodType, z } from "zod";

export function validateSchemaMiddleware<T>(
  schema: ZodType<T>,
  property: "body" | "params" | "query" = "body",
) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
    const result = schema.safeParse(req[property]);

    if (!result.success) {
      res.status(400).json({
        error: {
          code: "VALIDATION_ERROR",
          message: result.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Dados inválidos.",
          details: z.flattenError(result.error),
        },
      });
      return;
    }

    if (property === "query") {
      Object.assign(req.query, result.data);
    } else if (property === "params") {
      Object.assign(req.params, result.data);
    } else {
      req.body = result.data;
    }

    next();
  };
}

ARQUIVO [024/252]: Backend/core/security/security.utils.ts

import crypto from "node:crypto";
import * as bcrypt from "bcrypt";

export class SecurityUtils {
  static async hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string> {
    const saltRounds = 10;
    return bcrypt.hash(password, saltRounds);
  }

  static async comparePassword(password: string, hashedPassword: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return bcrypt.compare(password, hashedPassword);
  }

  static generatePublicId(): string {
    return crypto.randomUUID();
  }

  static generateOrderCode(): string {
    const now = new Date();
    const y = now.getFullYear();
    const m = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const d = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    const random = crypto.randomInt(0, 10000).toString().padStart(4, "0");
    return `ORD-${y}${m}${d}-${random}`;
  }
}

ARQUIVO [025/252]: Backend/core/storage/bucket.provider.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 95

import { S3Client, PutObjectCommand, DeleteObjectCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { env } from "../config/env.js";

export interface UploadFileInput {
  key: string;
  body: Buffer;
  contentType: string;
}

export interface UploadResult {
  key: string;
  url: string;
}

export interface IStorageProvider {
  upload(input: UploadFileInput): Promise<UploadResult>;
  delete(keyOrUrl: string): Promise<void>;
}

function getS3Client(): S3Client {
  if (!env.BUCKET_URL || !env.BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY || !env.BUCKET_SECRET_KEY || !env.BUCKET_NAME) {
    throw new Error("STORAGE_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  }

  return new S3Client({
    region: env.BUCKET_REGION,
    endpoint: env.BUCKET_URL,
    forcePathStyle: true,
    credentials: {
      accessKeyId: env.BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY,
      secretAccessKey: env.BUCKET_SECRET_KEY,
    },
  });
}

export function buildPublicAssetUrl(key: string): string {
  const cleanKey = key.replace(/^\/+/, "");
  if (env.BUCKET_PUBLIC_URL) {
    return `${env.BUCKET_PUBLIC_URL.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/${cleanKey}`;
  }
  if (env.BUCKET_URL && env.BUCKET_NAME) {
    return `${env.BUCKET_URL.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/${env.BUCKET_NAME}/${cleanKey}`;
  }
  return cleanKey;
}

export function extractStorageKey(keyOrUrl: string): string {
  if (!keyOrUrl.startsWith("http://") && !keyOrUrl.startsWith("https://")) {
    return keyOrUrl.replace(/^\/+/, "");
  }

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(keyOrUrl);
    const pathname = parsed.pathname.replace(/^\/+/, "");
    const bucketPrefix = `${env.BUCKET_NAME}/`;
    return pathname.startsWith(bucketPrefix) ? pathname.slice(bucketPrefix.length) : pathname;
  } catch {
    return keyOrUrl;
  }
}

export const r2StorageProvider: IStorageProvider = {
  async upload({ key, body, contentType }): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const client = getS3Client();
    const command = new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: env.BUCKET_NAME,
      Key: key,
      Body: body,
      ContentType: contentType,
    });

    await client.send(command);

    return {
      key,
      url: buildPublicAssetUrl(key),
    };
  },

  async delete(keyOrUrl: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!env.BUCKET_URL || !env.BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY || !env.BUCKET_SECRET_KEY || !env.BUCKET_NAME) {
      return;
    }

    const key = extractStorageKey(keyOrUrl);
    const client = getS3Client();
    const command = new DeleteObjectCommand({
      Bucket: env.BUCKET_NAME,
      Key: key,
    });

    await client.send(command);
  },
};

ARQUIVO [026/252]: Backend/core/storage/image.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 40

import sharp from "sharp";

export interface ProcessedImages {
  large: Buffer;
  contentType: string;
}

export interface ImageDimensions {
  large: { width: number; height: number };
}

const DEFAULT_DIMENSIONS: ImageDimensions = {
  large: { width: 512, height: 512 },
};

export const imageService = {
  async processAvatar(
    buffer: Buffer,
    dimensions: ImageDimensions = DEFAULT_DIMENSIONS,
  ): Promise<ProcessedImages> {
    const largeBuffer = await sharp(buffer)
      .resize(dimensions.large.width, dimensions.large.height, {
        fit: "cover",
        position: "center",
      })
      .webp({ quality: 85 })
      .toBuffer();

    return {
      large: largeBuffer,
      contentType: "image/webp",
    };
  },

  generateAvatarKey(entityPrefix: string, publicId: string): string {
    const timestamp = Date.now();
    return `${entityPrefix}/${publicId}/${timestamp}-lg.webp`;
  },
};

import type { Express, Response } from "express";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import swaggerUi from "swagger-ui-express";
import swaggerJsdoc from "swagger-jsdoc";

const currentDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const isDist = currentDir.includes(`${path.sep}dist${path.sep}`);

const swaggerRouteGlobs = isDist
  ? [path.resolve(currentDir, "..", "..", "api", "**", "*.routes.js")]
  : [path.resolve(currentDir, "..", "..", "api", "**", "*.routes.ts")];

const swaggerOptions = {
  definition: {
    openapi: "3.0.0",
    info: {
      title: "Black Amber Coffee API",
      description: "API RESTful Clean MVC Feature-Driven com PostgreSQL",
      version: "2.0.0",
    },
    servers: [
      { url: "/v1/api", description: "API v1 (Principal)" },
      { url: "/api", description: "API Alias" },
    ],
    components: {
      securitySchemes: {
        bearerAuth: {
          type: "http",
          scheme: "bearer",
          bearerFormat: "JWT",
          description: "Insira o accessToken JWT obtido no login",
        },
      },
    },
  },
  apis: swaggerRouteGlobs,
};

const swaggerSpec = swaggerJsdoc(swaggerOptions);

export function setupSwagger(app: Express): void {
  const uiOptions = { swaggerOptions: { defaultModelsExpandDepth: -1 } };
  app.use("/v1/docs", swaggerUi.serve, swaggerUi.setup(swaggerSpec, uiOptions));
  app.use("/api/docs", swaggerUi.serve, swaggerUi.setup(swaggerSpec, uiOptions));
  app.get("/v1/docs.json", (_req, res: Response) => {
    res.json(swaggerSpec);
  });
  app.get("/api/docs.json", (_req, res: Response) => {
    res.json(swaggerSpec);
  });
}

ARQUIVO [028/252]: Backend/src/server.ts
SEÇÃO: 2. Back-end — Servidor e Núcleo (Backend Core) | LINHAS: 100

import express, { type Express, type NextFunction, type Request, type Response } from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import { env } from "../core/config/env.js";
import { runMigrations } from "../core/database/migrator.js";
import { runSeed } from "../core/database/seeder.js";
import { globalErrorHandler, logger } from "../core/errors/index.js";
import {
  bodySizeLimit,
  rateLimit,
  secureHeaders,
  securityLogger,
} from "../core/middlewares/security.middleware.js";
import { setupSwagger } from "../core/swagger/swagger.js";
import { apiRouter } from "../api/index.js";
import { HealthController } from "../api/health/controllers/health.controller.js";

export function createApp(options: { enableRateLimit?: boolean } = {}): Express {
  const app = express();
  const { enableRateLimit = true } = options;

  app.set("trust proxy", 1);

  app.use(
    cors({
      origin: env.corsOrigins,
      credentials: env.corsOrigins !== "*",
    }),
  );
  app.use(express.json({ limit: "1mb" }));

  app.use(secureHeaders);
  if (enableRateLimit) {
    app.use(rateLimit);
  }
  app.use(bodySizeLimit);
  app.use(securityLogger);

  app.use((req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const start = process.hrtime.bigint();
    res.on("finish", () => {
      if (req.originalUrl === "/" && req.method === "GET") return;
      const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - start) / 1_000_000;
      logger.info(`${req.method} ${req.originalUrl} -> ${res.statusCode} (${durationMs.toFixed(2)}ms)`);
    });
    next();
  });

  app.get("/", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    res.status(200).json({
      status: "online",
      message: "Black Amber Coffee API está rodando com sucesso.",
      health: "/v1/api/health",
      docs: "/v1/docs",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  });

  app.get("/health", (req: Request, res: Response) => void HealthController.check(req, res));

  setupSwagger(app);

  app.use("/v1/api", apiRouter);
  app.use("/api", apiRouter);

  app.use((_req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    next(new Error("NOT_FOUND"));
  });

  app.use(globalErrorHandler);

  return app;
}

const app = createApp();

async function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await runMigrations();
    if (env.AUTO_SEED) {
      await runSeed();
    }

    app.listen(env.PORT, () => {
      const publicPort = env.PUBLIC_PORT ?? env.PORT;
      console.log(`[Backend] Servidor rodando na porta ${env.PORT}`);
      console.log(`[Backend] Health Check: http://localhost:${publicPort}/v1/api/health`);
      console.log(`[Backend] Swagger UI: http://localhost:${publicPort}/v1/docs`);
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Backend] Falha fatal ao iniciar o servidor:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

if (!env.isTest) {
  void bootstrap();
}

export default app;

    const emptyRes = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/register").send({});
    expect(emptyRes.status).toBe(400);

    const weakRes = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/register").send({
      name: "Teste",
      email: "novo@test.com",
      password: "fraca",
    });
    expect(weakRes.status).toBe(400);
  });

  it("POST /v1/api/auth/register retorna 201 com dados válidos", async () => {
    mockPoolQuery
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [] }) // clients check
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [] }) // workers check
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [activeClientRow] }); // insert

    const res = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/register").send({
      name: "Test Client",
      email: "novo@test.com",
      password: "Senha123",
    });

    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.data.email).toBe("user@test.com");
  });

  it("POST /v1/api/auth/register retorna 409 se email já existir", async () => {
    mockPoolQuery.mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [activeClientRow] });

    const res = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/register").send({
      name: "Test Client",
      email: "user@test.com",
      password: "Senha123",
    });

    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
  });

  it("POST /v1/api/auth/login retorna 401 com senha errada", async () => {
    mockPoolQuery.mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [activeClientRow] });

    const res = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/login").send({
      email: "user@test.com",
      password: "Errada123",
    });

    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("POST /v1/api/auth/login retorna 403 se a conta estiver desativada (is_active = false)", async () => {
    mockPoolQuery.mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [inactiveClientRow] });

    const res = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/login").send({
      email: "blocked@test.com",
      password: "Senha123",
    });

    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect(res.body.error.code).toBe("ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED");
  });

  it("POST /v1/api/auth/login retorna 201 com tokens quando credenciais válidas", async () => {
    mockPoolQuery.mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [activeClientRow] });

    const res = await request(app).post("/v1/api/auth/login").send({
      email: "user@test.com",
      password: "Senha123",
    });

    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.data).toHaveProperty("accessToken");
    expect(res.body.data).toHaveProperty("refreshToken");
  });
});

describe("Admin Toggle Status (Workers & Users)", () => {
  it("PATCH /v1/api/workers/:publicId/status alterna isActive", async () => {
    const jwt = new JWTService();
    const adminToken = jwt.generateToken({
      id: activeAdminWorkerRow.id,
      email: activeAdminWorkerRow.email,
      publicId: activeAdminWorkerRow.public_id,
      role: "gerente",
      isAdmin: true,
    });

    const targetWorker = { ...activeAdminWorkerRow, public_id: "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb", is_active: true };
    const toggledWorker = { ...targetWorker, is_active: false };

    mockPoolQuery
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [{ is_active: true }] }) // AuthMiddleware status check
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [targetWorker] }) // WorkerModel.findByPublicId
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [toggledWorker] }); // WorkerModel.updateByPublicId

    const res = await request(app)
      .patch(`/v1/api/workers/${targetWorker.public_id}/status`)
      .set("Authorization", `Bearer ${adminToken}`)
      .send({});

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.isActive).toBe(false);
  });

  it("PATCH /v1/api/users/:publicId/status alterna isActive do cliente", async () => {
    const jwt = new JWTService();
    const adminToken = jwt.generateToken({
      id: activeAdminWorkerRow.id,
      email: activeAdminWorkerRow.email,
      publicId: activeAdminWorkerRow.public_id,
      role: "gerente",
      isAdmin: true,
    });

    mockPoolQuery
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [{ is_active: true }] }) // AuthMiddleware status check
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [activeClientRow] }) // UserModel.findByPublicId
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ rows: [{ ...activeClientRow, is_active: false }] }); // UserModel.updateByPublicId

    const res = await request(app)
      .patch(`/v1/api/users/${activeClientRow.public_id}/status`)
      .set("Authorization", `Bearer ${adminToken}`)
      .send({});

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.isActive).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("Storage Public URL & ASVS Security", () => {
  it("buildPublicAssetUrl gera URL pública completa para imagens", () => {
    const url = buildPublicAssetUrl("products/123/foto-lg.webp");
    expect(url).toMatch(/^http:\/\/localhost:9000\/test-bucket\/products\/123\/foto-lg\.webp$/);
  });

  it("Headers ASVS estão presentes e bodySizeLimit retorna 413 > 1MB (mas libera multipart/form-data)", async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get("/v1/api/health");
    expect(res.headers["x-content-type-options"]).toBe("nosniff");
    expect(res.headers["x-frame-options"]).toBe("DENY");

    const mockJson = vi.fn();
    const mockStatus = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ json: mockJson });
    const mockNext = vi.fn();

    bodySizeLimit(
      { headers: { "content-length": "2000000", "content-type": "application/json" } } as unknown as Request,
      { status: mockStatus } as unknown as Response,
      mockNext,
    );
    expect(mockStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(413);
    expect(mockNext).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    const mockNextMultipart = vi.fn();
    bodySizeLimit(
      { headers: { "content-length": "2000000", "content-type": "multipart/form-data; boundary=---123" } } as unknown as Request,
      { status: mockStatus } as unknown as Response,
      mockNextMultipart,
    );
    expect(mockNextMultipart).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

export interface DashboardAnalyticsDTO {
  totalOrdersToday: number;
  totalRevenueToday: number;
  revenueChange: number;
  ordersByStatus: Array<{ status: string; count: number }>;
  topProducts: Array<{
    productId: number;
    productName: string;
    totalSold: number;
    totalRevenue: number;
  }>;
  totalProducts: number;
  totalClients: number;
  totalWorkers: number;
  lowStockItems: number;
  pendingOrders: number;
  inProgressOrders: number;
}

ARQUIVO [032/252]: Backend/api/analytics/models/analytics.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 104
========================================================================================
import { pool } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import { OrderStatus } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import type { DashboardAnalyticsDTO } from "../dtos/analytics.dto.js";

export class AnalyticsModel {
  static async fetchDashboard(): Promise<DashboardAnalyticsDTO> {
    const now = new Date();
    const todayStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
    const yesterdayStart = new Date(todayStart.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    const [
      ordersTodayRes,
      ordersYesterdayRes,
      ordersByStatusRes,
      topProductsRes,
      productsCountRes,
      clientsCountRes,
      workersCountRes,
      lowStockRes,
    ] = await Promise.all([
      pool.query<{ status: string; total_amount: string }>(
        `SELECT status::text AS status, total_amount FROM public.orders WHERE created_at >= $1`,
        [todayStart],
      ),
      pool.query<{ total_amount: string }>(
        `SELECT total_amount FROM public.orders WHERE created_at >= $1 AND created_at < $2`,
        [yesterdayStart, todayStart],
      ),
      pool.query<{ status: string; count: string }>(
        `SELECT status::text AS status, COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM public.orders GROUP BY status`,
      ),
      pool.query<{
        product_id: number;
        product_name: string | null;
        total_sold: string;
        total_revenue: string;
      }>(
        `SELECT
           oi.product_id,
           p.name AS product_name,
           COALESCE(SUM(oi.quantity), 0)::text AS total_sold,
           COALESCE(SUM(oi.unit_price * oi.quantity), 0)::text AS total_revenue
         FROM public.order_items oi
         LEFT JOIN public.products p ON p.id = oi.product_id
         GROUP BY oi.product_id, p.name
         ORDER BY SUM(oi.quantity) DESC
         LIMIT 5`,
      ),
      pool.query<{ total: string }>(`SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS total FROM public.products`),
      pool.query<{ total: string }>(`SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS total FROM public.clients`),
      pool.query<{ total: string }>(`SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS total FROM public.workers`),
      pool.query<{ total: string }>(
        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS total FROM public.inventory_items WHERE quantity <= min_quantity`,
      ),
    ]);

    const totalOrdersToday = ordersTodayRes.rows.length;
    const totalRevenueToday = ordersTodayRes.rows.reduce(
      (sum, row) => sum + Number(row.total_amount),
      0,
    );
    const totalRevenueYesterday = ordersYesterdayRes.rows.reduce(
      (sum, row) => sum + Number(row.total_amount),
      0,
    );

    const revenueChange =
      totalRevenueYesterday > 0
        ? ((totalRevenueToday - totalRevenueYesterday) / totalRevenueYesterday) * 100
        : totalRevenueToday > 0
          ? 100
          : 0;

    const pendingOrders = ordersTodayRes.rows.filter(
      (r) => r.status === OrderStatus.CRIADO,
    ).length;
    const inProgressOrders = ordersTodayRes.rows.filter(
      (r) => r.status === OrderStatus.EM_PREPARO,
    ).length;

    return {
      totalOrdersToday,
      totalRevenueToday: Math.round(totalRevenueToday * 100) / 100,
      revenueChange: Math.round(revenueChange * 100) / 100,
      ordersByStatus: ordersByStatusRes.rows.map((r) => ({
        status: r.status,
        count: Number(r.count),
      })),
      topProducts: topProductsRes.rows.map((r) => ({
        productId: Number(r.product_id),
        productName: r.product_name ?? "Produto",
        totalSold: Number(r.total_sold),
        totalRevenue: Number(r.total_revenue),
      })),
      totalProducts: Number(productsCountRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0),
      totalClients: Number(clientsCountRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0),
      totalWorkers: Number(workersCountRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0),
      lowStockItems: Number(lowStockRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0),
      pendingOrders,
      inProgressOrders,
    };
  }
}

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { AuthService } from "../services/auth.service.js";
import type {
  RegisterInput,
  LoginInput,
  RefreshTokenInput,
  LogoutInput,
  SendPasswordResetInput,
  CheckPasswordResetInput,
  ResetPasswordInput,
} from "../dtos/auth.dto.js";

export class AuthController {
  static async register(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.register(req.body as RegisterInput);
      res.status(201).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async login(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.login(req.body as LoginInput);
      res.status(201).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async refreshToken(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.refreshToken(req.body as RefreshTokenInput);
      res.status(200).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async logout(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.logout(req.body as LogoutInput);
      res.status(200).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async sendPasswordReset(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.sendPasswordReset(req.body as SendPasswordResetInput);
      res.status(200).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async checkPasswordReset(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.checkPasswordReset(req.body as CheckPasswordResetInput);
      res.status(200).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async resetPassword(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await AuthService.resetPassword(req.body as ResetPasswordInput);
      res.status(200).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

import type * as z from "zod";
import type {
  RegisterClientSchema,
  LoginSchema,
  RefreshTokenSchema,
  LogoutSchema,
  SendPasswordResetSchema,
  CheckPasswordResetSchema,
  ResetPasswordSchema,
} from "../validators/auth.validator.js";

export type RegisterInput = z.infer<typeof RegisterClientSchema>;
export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof LoginSchema>;
export type RefreshTokenInput = z.infer<typeof RefreshTokenSchema>;
export type LogoutInput = z.infer<typeof LogoutSchema>;
export type SendPasswordResetInput = z.infer<typeof SendPasswordResetSchema>;
export type CheckPasswordResetInput = z.infer<typeof CheckPasswordResetSchema>;
export type ResetPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof ResetPasswordSchema>;

export interface ProfileResponse {
  fullName: string;
  phone: string | null;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface RegisterResponse {
  data: {
    publicId: string;
    email: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    profile: ProfileResponse;
  };
}

export interface LoginResponse {
  data: {
    accessToken: string;
    refreshToken: string;
    user: {
      publicId: string;
      email: string;
      userType: "user" | "worker";
      role?: string | null;
      profile: ProfileResponse;
    };
  };
}

export interface RefreshTokenResponse {
  data: {
    accessToken: string;
    refreshToken: string;
  };
}

export interface LogoutResponse {
  data: {
    success: boolean;
  };
}

export interface SendPasswordResetResponse {
  email: string;
  userTokenId: string | null;
  expiresAt: Date;
}

export interface CheckPasswordResetResponse {
  resetToken: string;
}

export interface ResetPasswordResponse {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  user: {
    publicId: string;
    email: string;
    profile: ProfileResponse;
  };
}
