ARQUIVO [037/252]: Backend/api/auth/models/auth.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 213
========================================================================================
import { pool, withTransaction } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import type { WorkerRole } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";

export interface AuthUserRecord {
  id: number;
  publicId: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
  phone: string | null;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  isActive: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface AuthEntity {
  type: "user" | "worker";
  user: AuthUserRecord;
  role?: WorkerRole;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
}

export interface PasswordResetTokenRecord {
  id: number;
  email: string;
  code: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
  used: boolean;
}

interface ClientRow {
  id: number;
  public_id: string;
  full_name: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
  phone: string | null;
  avatar_url: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

interface WorkerRow extends ClientRow {
  role: WorkerRole;
  is_admin: boolean;
}

function mapClientRow(row: ClientRow): AuthUserRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    publicId: row.public_id,
    name: row.full_name,
    email: row.email,
    password: row.password,
    phone: row.phone,
    avatarUrl: row.avatar_url,
    isActive: row.is_active,
    createdAt: row.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

export class AuthModel {
  static async createClient(input: {
    name: string;
    email: string;
    password: string;
    phone?: string;
  }): Promise<AuthUserRecord> {
    const result = await pool.query<ClientRow>(
      `INSERT INTO public.clients (email, password, full_name, phone)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
       RETURNING id, public_id, full_name, email, password, phone, avatar_url, is_active, created_at, updated_at`,
      [input.email, input.password, input.name, input.phone ?? null],
    );
    return mapClientRow(result.rows[0]);
  }

  static async findByEmail(email: string): Promise<AuthEntity | null> {
    const clientRes = await pool.query<ClientRow>(
      `SELECT id, public_id, full_name, email, password, phone, avatar_url, is_active, created_at, updated_at
       FROM public.clients WHERE email = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [email],
    );

    if (clientRes.rows.length > 0) {
      return { type: "user", user: mapClientRow(clientRes.rows[0]) };
    }

    const workerRes = await pool.query<WorkerRow>(
      `SELECT id, public_id, full_name, email, password, phone, avatar_url, is_active, role, is_admin, created_at, updated_at
       FROM public.workers WHERE email = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [email],
    );

    if (workerRes.rows.length > 0) {
      const w = workerRes.rows[0];
      return {
        type: "worker",
        role: w.role,
        isAdmin: w.is_admin,
        user: mapClientRow(w),
      };
    }

    return null;
  }

  static async findByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<AuthEntity | null> {
    const clientRes = await pool.query<ClientRow>(
      `SELECT id, public_id, full_name, email, password, phone, avatar_url, is_active, created_at, updated_at
       FROM public.clients WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );

    if (clientRes.rows.length > 0) {
      return { type: "user", user: mapClientRow(clientRes.rows[0]) };
    }

    const workerRes = await pool.query<WorkerRow>(
      `SELECT id, public_id, full_name, email, password, phone, avatar_url, is_active, role, is_admin, created_at, updated_at
       FROM public.workers WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );

    if (workerRes.rows.length > 0) {
      const w = workerRes.rows[0];
      return {
        type: "worker",
        role: w.role,
        isAdmin: w.is_admin,
        user: mapClientRow(w),
      };
    }

    return null;
  }

  static async createPasswordReset(
    email: string,
    code: string,
    expiresAt: Date,
  ): Promise<{ id: number; email: string; code: string; expiresAt: Date }> {
    return withTransaction(async (client) => {
      await client.query(
        `UPDATE public.password_reset_tokens SET used = TRUE WHERE email = $1 AND used = FALSE`,
        [email],
      );

      const inserted = await client.query<{
        id: number;
        email: string;
        code: string;
        expires_at: Date;
      }>(
        `INSERT INTO public.password_reset_tokens (email, code, expires_at)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
         RETURNING id, email, code, expires_at`,
        [email, code, expiresAt],
      );

      const row = inserted.rows[0];
      return {
        id: row.id,
        email: row.email,
        code: row.code,
        expiresAt: row.expires_at,
      };
    });
  }

  static async getPasswordResetByCode(code: string): Promise<PasswordResetTokenRecord | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<{
      id: number;
      email: string;
      code: string;
      expires_at: Date;
      used: boolean;
    }>(
      `SELECT id, email, code, expires_at, used FROM public.password_reset_tokens WHERE code = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [code],
    );

    if (res.rows.length === 0) return null;
    const row = res.rows[0];
    return {
      id: row.id,
      email: row.email,
      code: row.code,
      expiresAt: row.expires_at,
      used: row.used,
    };
  }

  static async markPasswordResetAsUsed(code: string): Promise<void> {
    await pool.query(`UPDATE public.password_reset_tokens SET used = TRUE WHERE code = $1`, [code]);
  }

  static async updatePasswordByPublicId(
    publicId: string,
    entityType: "user" | "worker",
    hashedPassword: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const table = entityType === "worker" ? "public.workers" : "public.clients";
    await pool.query(`UPDATE ${table} SET password = $1 WHERE public_id = $2`, [
      hashedPassword,
      publicId,
    ]);
  }
}

ARQUIVO [038/252]: Backend/api/auth/routes/auth.routes.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 79
========================================================================================
import { Router } from "express";
import { validateSchemaMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/validation.middleware.js";
import { AuthController } from "../controllers/auth.controller.js";
import {
  RegisterClientSchema,
  LoginSchema,
  RefreshTokenSchema,
  LogoutSchema,
  SendPasswordResetSchema,
  CheckPasswordResetSchema,
  ResetPasswordSchema,
} from "../validators/auth.validator.js";

export const authRoutes = Router();

/**
 * @swagger
 * /auth/register:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Registrar novo cliente
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     responses:
 *       201:
 *         description: Cliente criado com sucesso
 */
authRoutes.post(
  "/register",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(RegisterClientSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.register(req, res, next),
);

/**
 * @swagger
 * /auth/login:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Login unificado (cliente ou funcionário)
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     responses:
 *       201:
 *         description: Tokens e dados do usuário
 *       403:
 *         description: Conta desativada
 */
authRoutes.post(
  "/login",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(LoginSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.login(req, res, next),
);

authRoutes.post(
  "/jwt/refresh-token",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(RefreshTokenSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.refreshToken(req, res, next),
);

authRoutes.post(
  "/logout",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(LogoutSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.logout(req, res, next),
);

authRoutes.post(
  "/forgotpassword/send",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(SendPasswordResetSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.sendPasswordReset(req, res, next),
);

authRoutes.post(
  "/forgotpassword/check",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(CheckPasswordResetSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.checkPasswordReset(req, res, next),
);

authRoutes.post(
  "/forgotpassword/reset",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(ResetPasswordSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void AuthController.resetPassword(req, res, next),
);

ARQUIVO [039/252]: Backend/api/auth/services/auth.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 263
========================================================================================
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { JWTService } from "../../../core/jwt/jwt.service.js";
import { SecurityUtils } from "../../../core/security/security.utils.js";
import { mailService } from "../../../core/mail/mail.service.js";
import { AuthModel } from "../models/auth.model.js";
import type {
  RegisterInput,
  RegisterResponse,
  LoginInput,
  LoginResponse,
  RefreshTokenInput,
  RefreshTokenResponse,
  LogoutInput,
  LogoutResponse,
  SendPasswordResetInput,
  SendPasswordResetResponse,
  CheckPasswordResetInput,
  CheckPasswordResetResponse,
  ResetPasswordInput,
  ResetPasswordResponse,
} from "../dtos/auth.dto.js";

const jwtService = new JWTService();

export class AuthService {
  static async register(data: RegisterInput): Promise<RegisterResponse> {
    const existing = await AuthModel.findByEmail(data.email);
    if (existing) {
      throw new Error("EMAIL_ALREADY_IN_USE");
    }

    const hashedPassword = await SecurityUtils.hashPassword(data.password);
    const created = await AuthModel.createClient({
      name: data.name,
      email: data.email,
      password: hashedPassword,
      phone: data.phone,
    });

    return {
      data: {
        publicId: created.publicId,
        email: created.email,
        createdAt: created.createdAt,
        updatedAt: created.updatedAt,
        profile: {
          fullName: created.name,
          phone: created.phone,
          avatarUrl: created.avatarUrl,
          createdAt: created.createdAt,
        },
      },
    };
  }

  static async login(data: LoginInput): Promise<LoginResponse> {
    const authEntity = await AuthModel.findByEmail(data.email);
    if (!authEntity) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_CREDENTIALS");
    }

    const isPasswordValid = await SecurityUtils.comparePassword(
      data.password,
      authEntity.user.password,
    );
    if (!isPasswordValid) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_CREDENTIALS");
    }

    if (!authEntity.user.isActive) {
      throw new Error("ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED");
    }

    const isWorker = authEntity.type === "worker";

    const accessToken = jwtService.generateToken({
      id: authEntity.user.id,
      email: authEntity.user.email,
      publicId: authEntity.user.publicId,
      ...(isWorker && { role: authEntity.role, isAdmin: authEntity.isAdmin }),
    });

    const refreshToken = jwtService.generateRefreshToken(authEntity.user.publicId);

    return {
      data: {
        accessToken,
        refreshToken,
        user: {
          publicId: authEntity.user.publicId,
          email: authEntity.user.email,
          userType: isWorker ? "worker" : "user",
          role: isWorker ? authEntity.role ?? null : null,
          profile: {
            fullName: authEntity.user.name,
            phone: authEntity.user.phone,
            avatarUrl: authEntity.user.avatarUrl,
            createdAt: authEntity.user.createdAt,
            updatedAt: authEntity.user.updatedAt,
          },
        },
      },
    };
  }

  static async refreshToken(data: RefreshTokenInput): Promise<RefreshTokenResponse> {
    const decoded = jwtService.verifyRefreshToken(data.refreshToken);
    if (!decoded) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN");
    }

    const authEntity = await AuthModel.findByPublicId(decoded.id);
    if (!authEntity) {
      throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    }

    if (!authEntity.user.isActive) {
      throw new Error("ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED");
    }

    const isWorker = authEntity.type === "worker";

    const accessToken = jwtService.generateToken({
      id: authEntity.user.id,
      email: authEntity.user.email,
      publicId: authEntity.user.publicId,
      ...(isWorker && { role: authEntity.role, isAdmin: authEntity.isAdmin }),
    });

    const refreshToken = jwtService.generateRefreshToken(authEntity.user.publicId);

    return {
      data: {
        accessToken,
        refreshToken,
      },
    };
  }

  static async logout(data: LogoutInput): Promise<LogoutResponse> {
    const decoded = jwtService.verifyRefreshToken(data.refreshToken);
    if (!decoded) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN");
    }

    const authEntity = await AuthModel.findByPublicId(decoded.id);
    if (!authEntity) {
      throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    }

    return {
      data: {
        success: true,
      },
    };
  }

  static async sendPasswordReset(data: SendPasswordResetInput): Promise<SendPasswordResetResponse> {
    const authEntity = await AuthModel.findByEmail(data.email);
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000);

    if (!authEntity) {
      return {
        email: data.email,
        userTokenId: null,
        expiresAt,
      };
    }

    const code = crypto.randomInt(100000, 999999).toString();
    const userToken = await AuthModel.createPasswordReset(data.email, code, expiresAt);

    await mailService.send({
      schema: "PASSWORD_RESET",
      to: authEntity.user.email,
      data: { code },
    });

    return {
      email: data.email,
      userTokenId: userToken.code,
      expiresAt,
    };
  }

  static async checkPasswordReset(data: CheckPasswordResetInput): Promise<CheckPasswordResetResponse> {
    const reset = await AuthModel.getPasswordResetByCode(data.code);
    if (!reset) {
      throw new Error("NOT_FOUND");
    }

    if (reset.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      throw new Error("EXPIRED_CODE");
    }

    if (reset.used) {
      throw new Error("CODE_ALREADY_USED");
    }

    const authEntity = await AuthModel.findByEmail(reset.email);
    if (!authEntity) {
      throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    }

    await AuthModel.markPasswordResetAsUsed(data.code);

    const resetToken = jwtService.generatePasswordResetToken({
      userId: authEntity.user.id,
      publicUserId: authEntity.user.publicId,
    });

    return { resetToken };
  }

  static async resetPassword(data: ResetPasswordInput): Promise<ResetPasswordResponse> {
    const decoded = jwtService.verifyPasswordResetToken(data.resetToken);
    if (!decoded) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_TOKEN");
    }

    const authEntity = await AuthModel.findByPublicId(decoded.publicUserId);
    if (!authEntity) {
      throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    }

    if (!authEntity.user.isActive) {
      throw new Error("ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED");
    }

    const hashedPassword = await SecurityUtils.hashPassword(data.newPassword);
    await AuthModel.updatePasswordByPublicId(
      authEntity.user.publicId,
      authEntity.type,
      hashedPassword,
    );

    const isWorker = authEntity.type === "worker";
    const accessToken = jwtService.generateToken({
      id: authEntity.user.id,
      email: authEntity.user.email,
      publicId: authEntity.user.publicId,
      ...(isWorker && { role: authEntity.role, isAdmin: authEntity.isAdmin }),
    });

    const refreshToken = jwtService.generateRefreshToken(authEntity.user.publicId);

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken,
      user: {
        publicId: authEntity.user.publicId,
        email: authEntity.user.email,
        profile: {
          fullName: authEntity.user.name,
          phone: authEntity.user.phone,
          avatarUrl: authEntity.user.avatarUrl,
          createdAt: authEntity.user.createdAt,
        },
      },
    };
  }
}

import * as z from "zod";

export const RegisterClientSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Nome é obrigatório."),
  email: z.email("E-mail inválido."),
  password: z
    .string()
    .min(6, "A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres.")
    .regex(/[A-Z]/, "A senha deve ter ao menos uma letra maiúscula.")
    .regex(/[0-9]/, "A senha deve ter ao menos um número."),
  phone: z.string().min(1, "Telefone inválido.").optional(),
});

export const LoginSchema = z.object({
  email: z.email("E-mail inválido."),
  password: z.string().min(6, "A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres."),
});

export const RefreshTokenSchema = z.object({
  refreshToken: z.string().min(1, "Refresh token é obrigatório."),
});

export const LogoutSchema = z.object({
  refreshToken: z.string().min(1, "Refresh token é obrigatório."),
  logoutAllDevices: z.boolean().default(false),
});

export const SendPasswordResetSchema = z.object({
  email: z.email("E-mail inválido.").min(6, "O e-mail deve ter ao menos 6 caracteres."),
});

export const CheckPasswordResetSchema = z.object({
  userTokenId: z.string().min(1, "Código de reset é obrigatório."),
  code: z.string().min(1, "Código de reset é obrigatório."),
});

export const ResetPasswordSchema = z
  .object({
    resetToken: z.string().min(1, "Token de reset é obrigatório."),
    newPassword: z
      .string()
      .min(6, "A senha deve ter ao menos 6 caracteres.")
      .regex(/[A-Z]/, "A senha deve ter ao menos uma letra maiúscula.")
      .regex(/[0-9]/, "A senha deve ter ao menos um número."),
    confirmNewPassword: z
      .string()
      .min(6, "A senha deve ter ao menos 6 caracteres."),
  })
  .refine((data) => data.newPassword === data.confirmNewPassword, {
    message: "As senhas não conferem.",
    path: ["confirmNewPassword"],
  });

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { CartService } from "../services/cart.service.js";
import type { AddCartItemInput, UpdateCartItemInput } from "../dtos/cart.dto.js";

export class CartController {
  static async getCart(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const cart = await CartService.getCart(publicId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: cart });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async addItem(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const cart = await CartService.addItem(publicId, req.body as AddCartItemInput);
      res.status(200).json({ data: cart });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateItem(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const productId = Number(req.params.productId);
      const cart = await CartService.updateItem(
        publicId,
        productId,
        req.body as UpdateCartItemInput,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: cart });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async removeItem(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const productId = Number(req.params.productId);
      const cart = await CartService.removeItem(publicId, productId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: cart });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async clearCart(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const cart = await CartService.clearCart(publicId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: cart });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

import type * as z from "zod";
import type { AddCartItemSchema, UpdateCartItemSchema } from "../validators/cart.validator.js";

export interface CartItemDTO {
  productId: number;
  productPublicId: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  imgUrl: string | null;
  price: number;
  category: string;
  quantity: number;
  observation: string | null;
  addedAt: string;
}

export interface CartDTO {

  itemCount: number;
  subtotal: number;
  items: CartItemDTO[];
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export type AddCartItemInput = z.infer<typeof AddCartItemSchema>;

ARQUIVO [043/252]: Backend/api/cart/models/cart.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 191
========================================================================================
import { pool, withTransaction } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import type { CartDTO, CartItemDTO } from "../dtos/cart.dto.js";

interface CartRow {
  id: number;
  client_id: number;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

interface CartItemQueryRow {
  product_id: number;
  product_public_id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  img_url: string | null;
  price: string | number;
  category: string;
  quantity: number;
  observation: string | null;
  added_at: Date;
}

function buildCartDTO(cartRow: CartRow, items: CartItemDTO[]): CartDTO {
  const itemCount = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  const subtotal = Math.round(items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price * item.quantity, 0) * 100) / 100;
  return {
    id: cartRow.id,
    itemCount,
    subtotal,
    items,
    createdAt: cartRow.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: cartRow.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

export class CartModel {
  private static async loadCartWithItems(cartRow: CartRow): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const res = await pool.query<CartItemQueryRow>(
      `SELECT
         ci.product_id,
         p.public_id AS product_public_id,
         p.name,
         p.description,
         p.img_url,
         p.price,
         p.category::text AS category,
         ci.quantity,
         ci.observation,
         ci.added_at
       FROM public.cart_items ci
       INNER JOIN public.products p ON p.id = ci.product_id
       WHERE ci.cart_id = $1
       ORDER BY ci.added_at ASC`,
      [cartRow.id],
    );

    const items: CartItemDTO[] = res.rows.map((r) => ({
      productId: r.product_id,
      productPublicId: r.product_public_id,
      name: r.name,
      description: r.description,
      imgUrl: r.img_url,
      price: Number(r.price),
      category: r.category,
      quantity: r.quantity,
      observation: r.observation,
      addedAt: r.added_at.toISOString(),
    }));

    return buildCartDTO(cartRow, items);
  }

  static async getOrCreateByClientId(clientId: number): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const existing = await pool.query<CartRow>(
      `SELECT id, client_id, created_at, updated_at FROM public.carts WHERE client_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [clientId],
    );

    if (existing.rows.length > 0) {
      return this.loadCartWithItems(existing.rows[0]);
    }

    const created = await pool.query<CartRow>(
      `INSERT INTO public.carts (client_id) VALUES ($1) RETURNING id, client_id, created_at, updated_at`,
      [clientId],
    );
    return buildCartDTO(created.rows[0], []);
  }

  static async addItem(
    cartId: number,
    productId: number,
    quantity: number,
    observation?: string | null,
  ): Promise<CartDTO> {
    await withTransaction(async (client) => {
      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO public.cart_items (cart_id, product_id, quantity, observation)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
         ON CONFLICT (cart_id, product_id) DO UPDATE
         SET quantity = public.cart_items.quantity + EXCLUDED.quantity,
             observation = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.observation, public.cart_items.observation)`,
        [cartId, productId, quantity, observation ?? null],
      );

      await client.query(`UPDATE public.carts SET updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [cartId]);
    });

    const cartRes = await pool.query<CartRow>(
      `SELECT id, client_id, created_at, updated_at FROM public.carts WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [cartId],
    );
    return this.loadCartWithItems(cartRes.rows[0]);
  }

  static async updateItem(
    cartId: number,
    productId: number,
    data: { quantity?: number; observation?: string | null },
  ): Promise<CartDTO | null> {
    const existing = await pool.query<{ quantity: number }>(
      `SELECT quantity FROM public.cart_items WHERE cart_id = $1 AND product_id = $2 LIMIT 1`,
      [cartId, productId],
    );

    if (existing.rows.length === 0) return null;

    await withTransaction(async (client) => {
      const updates: string[] = [];
      const values: unknown[] = [];

      if (data.quantity !== undefined) {
        values.push(data.quantity);
        updates.push(`quantity = $${values.length}`);
      }
      if (data.observation !== undefined) {
        values.push(data.observation);
        updates.push(`observation = $${values.length}`);
      }

      if (updates.length > 0) {
        values.push(cartId, productId);
        await client.query(
          `UPDATE public.cart_items
           SET ${updates.join(", ")}
           WHERE cart_id = $${values.length - 1} AND product_id = $${values.length}`,
          values,
        );
      }

      await client.query(`UPDATE public.carts SET updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [cartId]);
    });

    const cartRes = await pool.query<CartRow>(
      `SELECT id, client_id, created_at, updated_at FROM public.carts WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [cartId],
    );
    return this.loadCartWithItems(cartRes.rows[0]);
  }

  static async removeItem(cartId: number, productId: number): Promise<CartDTO> {
    await withTransaction(async (client) => {
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.cart_items WHERE cart_id = $1 AND product_id = $2`, [
        cartId,
        productId,
      ]);
      await client.query(`UPDATE public.carts SET updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [cartId]);
    });

    const cartRes = await pool.query<CartRow>(
      `SELECT id, client_id, created_at, updated_at FROM public.carts WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [cartId],
    );
    return this.loadCartWithItems(cartRes.rows[0]);
  }

  static async clearCart(cartId: number): Promise<CartDTO> {
    await withTransaction(async (client) => {
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.cart_items WHERE cart_id = $1`, [cartId]);
      await client.query(`UPDATE public.carts SET updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [cartId]);
    });

    const cartRes = await pool.query<CartRow>(
      `SELECT id, client_id, created_at, updated_at FROM public.carts WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [cartId],
    );
    return this.loadCartWithItems(cartRes.rows[0]);
  }
}

import { Router } from "express";
import { AuthMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/auth.middleware.js";
import { requireRole } from "../../../core/middlewares/permission.middleware.js";
import { validateSchemaMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/validation.middleware.js";
import { CartController } from "../controllers/cart.controller.js";
import { AddCartItemSchema, UpdateCartItemSchema } from "../validators/cart.validator.js";

export const cartRoutes = Router();

cartRoutes.use(AuthMiddleware);
cartRoutes.use(requireRole("user"));

cartRoutes.get("/", (req, res, next) => void CartController.getCart(req, res, next));

cartRoutes.post(
  "/items",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(AddCartItemSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void CartController.addItem(req, res, next),
);

cartRoutes.patch(
  "/items/:productId",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateCartItemSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void CartController.updateItem(req, res, next),
);

cartRoutes.delete("/items/:productId", (req, res, next) =>
  void CartController.removeItem(req, res, next),
);

cartRoutes.delete("/", (req, res, next) => void CartController.clearCart(req, res, next));

ARQUIVO [045/252]: Backend/api/cart/services/cart.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 55
========================================================================================
import { ProductModel } from "../../product/models/product.model.js";
import { UserModel } from "../../user/models/user.model.js";
import { CartModel } from "../models/cart.model.js";
import type { AddCartItemInput, CartDTO, UpdateCartItemInput } from "../dtos/cart.dto.js";

export class CartService {
  static async getCart(clientPublicId: string): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(clientPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    return CartModel.getOrCreateByClientId(clientId);
  }

  static async addItem(clientPublicId: string, data: AddCartItemInput): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(clientPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    const product = await ProductModel.findById(data.productId);
    if (!product) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    if (!product.isActive) throw new Error("PRODUCT_INACTIVE");

    const cart = await CartModel.getOrCreateByClientId(clientId);
    return CartModel.addItem(cart.id, data.productId, data.quantity ?? 1, data.observation);
  }

  static async updateItem(
    clientPublicId: string,
    productId: number,
    data: UpdateCartItemInput,
  ): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(clientPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    const cart = await CartModel.getOrCreateByClientId(clientId);
    const updated = await CartModel.updateItem(cart.id, productId, data);
    if (!updated) throw new Error("CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async removeItem(clientPublicId: string, productId: number): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(clientPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    const cart = await CartModel.getOrCreateByClientId(clientId);
    return CartModel.removeItem(cart.id, productId);
  }

  static async clearCart(clientPublicId: string): Promise<CartDTO> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(clientPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    const cart = await CartModel.getOrCreateByClientId(clientId);
    return CartModel.clearCart(cart.id);
  }
}

ARQUIVO [046/252]: Backend/api/cart/validators/cart.validator.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 18

import * as z from "zod";

export const AddCartItemSchema = z.object({
  productId: z.coerce.number().int().positive(),
  quantity: z.coerce.number().int().positive().default(1),
  observation: z.string().nullable().optional(),
});

export const UpdateCartItemSchema = z
  .object({
    quantity: z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional(),
    observation: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  })
  .refine(
    (data) => data.quantity !== undefined || data.observation !== undefined,
    "Informe ao menos um campo para atualização.",
  );

import type { Request, Response } from "express";
import { HealthService } from "../services/health.service.js";

export class HealthController {
  static async check(_req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> {
    const health = await HealthService.check();
    const statusCode = health.database === "connected" ? 200 : 503;
    res.status(statusCode).json(health);
  }

  static metrics(_req: Request, res: Response): void {
    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      collectedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      metrics: HealthService.getMetrics(),
    });
  }
}

ARQUIVO [049/252]: Backend/api/health/services/health.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 66
========================================================================================
import { checkDatabaseHealth } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import { env } from "../../../core/config/env.js";

export interface HealthStatus {
  status: "ok" | "degraded";
  database: "connected" | "disconnected";
  dbLatencyMs: number;
  timestamp: string;
  uptime: number;
  environment: string;
}

export interface SystemMetrics {
  uptime_seconds: number;
  memory_rss_mb: number;
  memory_heap_used_mb: number;
  memory_heap_total_mb: number;
  memory_external_mb: number;
  cpu_user_ms: number;
  cpu_system_ms: number;
  event_loop_lag_ms: number;
  node_version: string;
  environment: string;
}

let eventLoopLag = 0;

setInterval(() => {
  const start = Date.now();
  setImmediate(() => {
    eventLoopLag = Date.now() - start;
  });
}, 1000).unref();

export class HealthService {
  static async check(): Promise<HealthStatus> {
    const dbHealth = await checkDatabaseHealth();
    return {
      status: dbHealth.connected ? "ok" : "degraded",
      database: dbHealth.connected ? "connected" : "disconnected",
      dbLatencyMs: dbHealth.latencyMs,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      uptime: Math.floor(process.uptime()),
      environment: env.NODE_ENV,
    };
  }

  static getMetrics(): SystemMetrics {
    const mem = process.memoryUsage();
    const cpu = process.cpuUsage();

    return {
      uptime_seconds: Math.floor(process.uptime()),
      memory_rss_mb: parseFloat((mem.rss / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)),
      memory_heap_used_mb: parseFloat((mem.heapUsed / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)),
      memory_heap_total_mb: parseFloat((mem.heapTotal / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)),
      memory_external_mb: parseFloat((mem.external / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)),
      cpu_user_ms: parseFloat((cpu.user / 1000).toFixed(2)),
      cpu_system_ms: parseFloat((cpu.system / 1000).toFixed(2)),
      event_loop_lag_ms: eventLoopLag,
      node_version: process.version,
      environment: env.NODE_ENV,
    };
  }
}

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { InventoryService } from "../services/inventory.service.js";
import type { CreateInventoryInput, UpdateInventoryInput } from "../dtos/inventory.dto.js";

export class InventoryController {
  static async listAll(_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const items = await InventoryService.listAll();
      res.status(200).json({ data: items });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async getById(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const item = await InventoryService.getByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: item });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async create(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const item = await InventoryService.create(req.body as CreateInventoryInput);
      res.status(201).json({ data: item, message: "Item de inventário criado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async addStock(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const item = await InventoryService.addStock(req.body as CreateInventoryInput);
      res.status(200).json({ data: item, message: "Estoque atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async update(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const item = await InventoryService.update(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        req.body as UpdateInventoryInput,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: item, message: "Item de inventário atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async remove(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await InventoryService.deleteByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(204).send();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

ARQUIVO [053/252]: Backend/api/inventory/models/inventory.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 144
========================================================================================
import { pool } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import type { InventoryItemDTO } from "../dtos/inventory.dto.js";

interface InventoryRow {
  id: number;
  public_id: string;
  code: string | null;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  quantity: string | number;
  unit: string;
  min_quantity: string | number;
  img_url: string | null;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

function mapInventoryRow(row: InventoryRow): InventoryItemDTO {
  return {
    publicId: row.public_id,
    code: row.code,
    name: row.name,
    description: row.description,
    category: row.category,
    quantity: Number(row.quantity),
    unit: row.unit,
    minQuantity: Number(row.min_quantity),
    imgUrl: row.img_url,
    createdAt: row.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

const INVENTORY_FIELDS = `
  id, public_id, code, name, description, category, quantity, unit, min_quantity, img_url, created_at, updated_at
`;

export class InventoryModel {
  static async findAll(): Promise<InventoryItemDTO[]> {
    const res = await pool.query<InventoryRow>(
      `SELECT ${INVENTORY_FIELDS} FROM public.inventory_items ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
    );
    return res.rows.map(mapInventoryRow);
  }

  static async findByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<InventoryItemDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<InventoryRow>(
      `SELECT ${INVENTORY_FIELDS} FROM public.inventory_items WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );
    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapInventoryRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async findByCode(code: string): Promise<InventoryItemDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<InventoryRow>(
      `SELECT ${INVENTORY_FIELDS} FROM public.inventory_items WHERE code = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [code],
    );
    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapInventoryRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async create(input: {
    code: string | null;
    name: string;
    description: string | null;
    category: string | null;
    quantity: number;
    unit: string;
    minQuantity: number;
    imgUrl: string | null;
  }): Promise<InventoryItemDTO> {
    const res = await pool.query<InventoryRow>(
      `INSERT INTO public.inventory_items (code, name, description, category, quantity, unit, min_quantity, img_url)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)
       RETURNING ${INVENTORY_FIELDS}`,
      [
        input.code,
        input.name,
        input.description,
        input.category,
        input.quantity,
        input.unit,
        input.minQuantity,
        input.imgUrl,
      ],
    );
    return mapInventoryRow(res.rows[0]);
  }

  static async updateByPublicId(
    publicId: string,
    fields: {
      name?: string;
      code?: string | null;
      description?: string | null;
      category?: string | null;
      quantity?: number;
      unit?: string;
      minQuantity?: number;
      imgUrl?: string | null;
    },
  ): Promise<InventoryItemDTO | null> {
    const setClauses: string[] = [];
    const values: unknown[] = [];

    const pushField = (column: string, value: unknown) => {
      values.push(value);
      setClauses.push(`${column} = $${values.length}`);
    };

    if (fields.name !== undefined) pushField("name", fields.name);
    if (fields.code !== undefined) pushField("code", fields.code);
    if (fields.description !== undefined) pushField("description", fields.description);
    if (fields.category !== undefined) pushField("category", fields.category);
    if (fields.quantity !== undefined) pushField("quantity", fields.quantity);
    if (fields.unit !== undefined) pushField("unit", fields.unit);
    if (fields.minQuantity !== undefined) pushField("min_quantity", fields.minQuantity);
    if (fields.imgUrl !== undefined) pushField("img_url", fields.imgUrl);

    if (setClauses.length === 0) {
      return this.findByPublicId(publicId);
    }

    values.push(publicId);
    const res = await pool.query<InventoryRow>(
      `UPDATE public.inventory_items
       SET ${setClauses.join(", ")}
       WHERE public_id = $${values.length}
       RETURNING ${INVENTORY_FIELDS}`,
      values,
    );

    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapInventoryRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await pool.query(`DELETE FROM public.inventory_items WHERE public_id = $1`, [
      publicId,
    ]);
    return (res.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
  }
}

import { Router } from "express";
import { AuthMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/auth.middleware.js";
import { requireAdmin } from "../../../core/middlewares/permission.middleware.js";
import { validateSchemaMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/validation.middleware.js";

import { CreateInventorySchema, UpdateInventorySchema } from "../validators/inventory.validator.js";

export const inventoryRoutes = Router();

inventoryRoutes.use(AuthMiddleware);

inventoryRoutes.get("/", (req, res, next) => void InventoryController.listAll(req, res, next));
inventoryRoutes.get("/:publicId", (req, res, next) =>
  void InventoryController.getById(req, res, next),
);

inventoryRoutes.post(
  "/create",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(CreateInventorySchema),
  (req, res, next) => void InventoryController.create(req, res, next),
);

inventoryRoutes.post(
  "/add-stock",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(CreateInventorySchema),
  (req, res, next) => void InventoryController.addStock(req, res, next),
);

inventoryRoutes.put(
  "/:publicId",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateInventorySchema),
  (req, res, next) => void InventoryController.update(req, res, next),
);

inventoryRoutes.delete("/:publicId", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void InventoryController.remove(req, res, next),
);

ARQUIVO [055/252]: Backend/api/inventory/services/inventory.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 79
========================================================================================
import { InventoryModel } from "../models/inventory.model.js";
import type {
  CreateInventoryInput,
  InventoryItemDTO,
  UpdateInventoryInput,
} from "../dtos/inventory.dto.js";

function normalizeCode(code?: string): string | null {
  if (!code) return null;
  const trimmed = code.trim();
  return trimmed === "" ? null : trimmed;
}

export class InventoryService {
  static async listAll(): Promise<InventoryItemDTO[]> {
    return InventoryModel.findAll();
  }

  static async getByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<InventoryItemDTO> {
    const item = await InventoryModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!item) throw new Error("INVENTORY_NOT_FOUND");
    return item;
  }

  static async create(data: CreateInventoryInput): Promise<InventoryItemDTO> {
    return InventoryModel.create({
      code: normalizeCode(data.code),
      name: data.name.trim(),
      description: data.description?.trim() ?? null,
      category: data.category?.trim() ?? null,
      quantity: data.quantity ?? 0,
      unit: data.unit,
      minQuantity: data.minQuantity ?? 10,
      imgUrl: data.imgUrl ?? null,
    });
  }

  static async addStock(data: CreateInventoryInput): Promise<InventoryItemDTO> {
    const cleanCode = normalizeCode(data.code);
    if (cleanCode) {
      const existing = await InventoryModel.findByCode(cleanCode);
      if (existing) {
        const updated = await InventoryModel.updateByPublicId(existing.publicId, {
          name: data.name.trim(),
          quantity: existing.quantity + (data.quantity ?? 0),
          unit: data.unit,
        });
        if (!updated) throw new Error("INVENTORY_NOT_FOUND");
        return updated;
      }
    }
    return this.create(data);
  }

  static async update(publicId: string, data: UpdateInventoryInput): Promise<InventoryItemDTO> {
    const current = await InventoryModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("INVENTORY_NOT_FOUND");

    const updated = await InventoryModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, {
      name: data.name?.trim(),
      code: data.code !== undefined ? normalizeCode(data.code) : undefined,
      description: data.description,
      category: data.category,
      quantity: data.quantity,
      unit: data.unit,
      minQuantity: data.minQuantity,
      imgUrl: data.imgUrl,
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("INVENTORY_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<void> {
    const deleted = await InventoryModel.deleteByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!deleted) throw new Error("INVENTORY_NOT_FOUND");
  }
}

import * as z from "zod";

export const CreateInventorySchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Nome é obrigatório."),
  code: z.string().optional(),
  description: z.string().optional(),
  category: z.string().optional(),
  quantity: z.coerce.number().min(0).optional(),
  unit: z.string().min(1, "Unidade é obrigatória."),
  minQuantity: z.coerce.number().min(0).optional(),
  imgUrl: z.string().optional(),
});

export const UpdateInventorySchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  code: z.string().optional(),
  description: z.string().optional(),
  category: z.string().optional(),
  quantity: z.coerce.number().min(0).optional(),
  unit: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  minQuantity: z.coerce.number().min(0).optional(),
  imgUrl: z.string().optional(),
});

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { OrderService } from "../services/order.service.js";
import type { CreateOrderInput, UpdateOrderStatusInput } from "../dtos/order.dto.js";

export class OrderController {
  static async list(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");

      const isWorkerOrAdmin = req.user?.role !== undefined || req.user?.isAdmin === true;
      if (isWorkerOrAdmin) {
        const status = typeof req.query.status === "string" ? req.query.status : undefined;
        const orders = await OrderService.listForWorker(publicId, status);
        res.status(200).json({ data: orders });
        return;
      }

      const orders = await OrderService.listForUser(publicId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: orders });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async create(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");

      const isWorkerOrAdmin = req.user?.role !== undefined || req.user?.isAdmin === true;
      const body = req.body as CreateOrderInput;

      const order = isWorkerOrAdmin
        ? await OrderService.createForWorker(publicId, body)
        : await OrderService.createForUser(publicId, body);

      res.status(201).json({ data: order });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async getByPublicId(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");

      const order = await OrderService.getForWorker(String(req.params.publicId), publicId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: order });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateStatus(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const workerPublicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!workerPublicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");

      const { status } = req.body as UpdateOrderStatusInput;
      const updated = await OrderService.updateStatusByWorker(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        workerPublicId,
        status,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: updated });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async cancel(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");

      const isWorkerOrAdmin = req.user?.role !== undefined || req.user?.isAdmin === true;
      const updated = await OrderService.cancel(String(req.params.publicId), {
        publicId,
        isWorkerOrAdmin,
      });
      res.status(200).json({ data: updated });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async remove(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await OrderService.deleteByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(204).send();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

ARQUIVO [059/252]: Backend/api/order/models/order.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 215
========================================================================================
import type { PoolClient } from "pg";
import { pool, withTransaction } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import { OrderStatus, type OrderStatusType } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import type { OrderDTO, OrderItemDTO } from "../dtos/order.dto.js";

interface OrderAggRow {
  id: number;
  public_id: string;
  code: string;
  client_id: number | null;
  updated_by: number | null;
  status: OrderStatusType;
  total_amount: string | number;
  observation: string | null;
  payment_method: string | null;
  itens: OrderItemDTO[];
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

const BASE_ORDER_QUERY = `
  SELECT
    o.id,
    o.public_id,
    o.code,
    o.client_id,
    o.updated_by,
    o.status,
    o.total_amount,
    o.observation,
    o.created_at,
    o.updated_at,
    p.method::text AS payment_method,
    COALESCE(
      json_agg(
        json_build_object(
          'id', COALESCE(pr.id, oi.product_id),
          'name', COALESCE(pr.name, ''),
          'price', oi.unit_price::float8,
          'quantity', oi.quantity,
          'observation', oi.observation
        )
        ORDER BY oi.product_id
      ) FILTER (WHERE oi.product_id IS NOT NULL),
      '[]'::json
    ) AS itens
  FROM public.orders o
  LEFT JOIN public.payments p ON p.order_id = o.id
  LEFT JOIN public.order_items oi ON oi.order_id = o.id
  LEFT JOIN public.products pr ON pr.id = oi.product_id
`;

function mapOrderRow(row: OrderAggRow): OrderDTO {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    publicId: row.public_id,
    code: row.code,
    clientId: row.client_id,
    updatedBy: row.updated_by,
    status: row.status,
    totalPrice: Number(row.total_amount),
    paymentMethod: row.payment_method,
    itens: Array.isArray(row.itens) ? row.itens : [],
    observation: row.observation,
    createdAt: row.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

export class OrderModel {
  static async findAll(status?: OrderStatusType): Promise<OrderDTO[]> {
    const values: unknown[] = [];
    let whereClause = "";

    if (status) {
      values.push(status);
      whereClause = `WHERE o.status = $1::public.order_status`;
    }

    const res = await pool.query<OrderAggRow>(
      `${BASE_ORDER_QUERY}
       ${whereClause}
       GROUP BY o.id, p.method
       ORDER BY o.created_at DESC`,
      values,
    );

    return res.rows.map(mapOrderRow);
  }

  static async findByClientId(clientId: number): Promise<OrderDTO[]> {
    const res = await pool.query<OrderAggRow>(
      `${BASE_ORDER_QUERY}
       WHERE o.client_id = $1
       GROUP BY o.id, p.method
       ORDER BY o.created_at DESC`,
      [clientId],
    );

    return res.rows.map(mapOrderRow);
  }

  static async findByPublicId(publicId: string, client?: PoolClient): Promise<OrderDTO | null> {
    const executor = client ?? pool;
    const res = await executor.query<OrderAggRow>(
      `${BASE_ORDER_QUERY}
       WHERE o.public_id = $1
       GROUP BY o.id, p.method
       LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );

    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapOrderRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async create(input: {
    clientId: number | null;
    publicId: string;
    code: string;
    totalAmount: number;
    status: OrderStatusType;
    observation: string | null;
    items: Array<{
      productId: number;
      quantity: number;
      unitPrice: number;
      observation?: string | null;
    }>;
    paymentMethod: string | null;
    actorWorkerId?: number | null;
  }): Promise<OrderDTO> {
    return withTransaction(async (client) => {
      const orderRes = await client.query<{ id: number; public_id: string }>(
        `INSERT INTO public.orders (public_id, code, client_id, total_amount, status, observation)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::public.order_status, $6)
         RETURNING id, public_id`,
        [
          input.publicId,
          input.code,
          input.clientId,
          input.totalAmount.toFixed(2),
          input.status,
          input.observation,
        ],
      );

      const orderId = orderRes.rows[0].id;
      const orderPublicId = orderRes.rows[0].public_id;

      for (const item of input.items) {
        await client.query(
          `INSERT INTO public.order_items (order_id, product_id, quantity, unit_price, observation)
           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
          [orderId, item.productId, item.quantity, item.unitPrice.toFixed(2), item.observation ?? null],
        );
      }

      if (input.paymentMethod) {
        await client.query(
          `INSERT INTO public.payments (order_id, amount, method, status)
           VALUES ($1, $2, $3::public.payment_method, 'pendente')`,
          [orderId, input.totalAmount.toFixed(2), input.paymentMethod],
        );
      }

      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO public.order_status_history (order_id, previous_status, new_status, changed_by_worker)
         VALUES ($1, $2::public.order_status, $3::public.order_status, $4)`,
        [orderId, input.status, input.status, input.actorWorkerId ?? null],
      );

      const created = await this.findByPublicId(orderPublicId, client);
      if (!created) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");
      return created;
    });
  }

  static async updateStatus(
    publicId: string,
    previousStatus: OrderStatusType,
    newStatus: OrderStatusType,
    workerId: number | null,
  ): Promise<OrderDTO | null> {
    return withTransaction(async (client) => {
      const res = await client.query<{ id: number }>(
        `UPDATE public.orders
         SET status = $1::public.order_status,
             updated_by = COALESCE($2, updated_by)
         WHERE public_id = $3
         RETURNING id`,
        [newStatus, workerId, publicId],
      );

      if (res.rows.length === 0) return null;

      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO public.order_status_history (order_id, previous_status, new_status, changed_by_worker)
         VALUES ($1, $2::public.order_status, $3::public.order_status, $4)`,
        [res.rows[0].id, previousStatus, newStatus, workerId],
      );

      return this.findByPublicId(publicId, client);
    });
  }

  static async cancel(publicId: string, previousStatus: OrderStatusType): Promise<OrderDTO | null> {
    return this.updateStatus(publicId, previousStatus, OrderStatus.CANCELADO, null);
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await pool.query(`DELETE FROM public.orders WHERE public_id = $1`, [publicId]);
    return (res.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
  }
}

import { Router } from "express";
import { AuthMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/auth.middleware.js";
import { requireAdmin, requireWorker } from "../../../core/middlewares/permission.middleware.js";
import { validateSchemaMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/validation.middleware.js";
import { OrderController } from "../controllers/order.controller.js";
import { CreateOrderSchema, UpdateOrderStatusSchema } from "../validators/order.validator.js";

export const orderRoutes = Router();

orderRoutes.use(AuthMiddleware);

orderRoutes.get("/", (req, res, next) => void OrderController.list(req, res, next));

orderRoutes.post(
  "/",
  validateSchemaMiddleware(CreateOrderSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void OrderController.create(req, res, next),
);

orderRoutes.get("/:publicId", requireWorker(), (req, res, next) =>
  void OrderController.getByPublicId(req, res, next),
);

orderRoutes.patch(
  "/:publicId/status",
  requireWorker(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateOrderStatusSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void OrderController.updateStatus(req, res, next),
);

orderRoutes.post("/:publicId/cancel", (req, res, next) =>
  void OrderController.cancel(req, res, next),
);

orderRoutes.delete("/:publicId", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void OrderController.remove(req, res, next),
);

ARQUIVO [061/252]: Backend/api/order/services/order.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 185
========================================================================================
import { OrderStatus, type OrderStatusType } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import { SecurityUtils } from "../../../core/security/security.utils.js";
import { ProductModel } from "../../product/models/product.model.js";
import { UserModel } from "../../user/models/user.model.js";
import { WorkerModel } from "../../worker/models/worker.model.js";
import { OrderModel } from "../models/order.model.js";
import type { CreateOrderInput, OrderDTO } from "../dtos/order.dto.js";

const ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Record<OrderStatusType, readonly OrderStatusType[]> = {
  [OrderStatus.CRIADO]: [OrderStatus.EM_PREPARO, OrderStatus.CANCELADO],
  [OrderStatus.EM_PREPARO]: [OrderStatus.CRIADO, OrderStatus.PRONTO, OrderStatus.CANCELADO],
  [OrderStatus.PRONTO]: [OrderStatus.FINALIZADO, OrderStatus.CANCELADO],
  [OrderStatus.FINALIZADO]: [],
  [OrderStatus.CANCELADO]: [],
};

export class OrderService {
  static async listForWorker(workerPublicId: string, statusFilter?: string): Promise<OrderDTO[]> {
    const worker = await WorkerModel.findIdByPublicId(workerPublicId);
    if (!worker) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    if (!worker.isActive) throw new Error("WORKER_INACTIVE");

    if (statusFilter) {
      if (!OrderStatus.isValid(statusFilter)) throw new Error("INVALID_STATUS");
      return OrderModel.findAll(statusFilter);
    }

    return OrderModel.findAll();
  }

  static async listForUser(userPublicId: string): Promise<OrderDTO[]> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(userPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    return OrderModel.findByClientId(clientId);
  }

  static async getForWorker(publicId: string, workerPublicId: string): Promise<OrderDTO> {
    const worker = await WorkerModel.findIdByPublicId(workerPublicId);
    if (!worker) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    if (!worker.isActive) throw new Error("WORKER_INACTIVE");

    const order = await OrderModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!order) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");
    return order;
  }

  static async createForUser(userPublicId: string, data: CreateOrderInput): Promise<OrderDTO> {
    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(userPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    let total = 0;
    const preparedItems: Array<{
      productId: number;
      quantity: number;
      unitPrice: number;
      observation?: string | null;
    }> = [];

    for (const item of data.items) {
      const product = await ProductModel.findById(item.productId);
      if (!product) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
      if (!product.isActive) throw new Error("PRODUCT_INACTIVE");

      total += product.price * item.quantity;
      preparedItems.push({
        productId: item.productId,
        quantity: item.quantity,
        unitPrice: product.price,
        observation: item.observation ?? null,
      });
    }

    return OrderModel.create({
      clientId,
      publicId: SecurityUtils.generatePublicId(),
      code: SecurityUtils.generateOrderCode(),
      totalAmount: total,
      status: OrderStatus.CRIADO,
      observation: data.observation ?? null,
      items: preparedItems,
      paymentMethod: data.paymentMethod ?? null,
    });
  }

  static async createForWorker(workerPublicId: string, data: CreateOrderInput): Promise<OrderDTO> {
    const worker = await WorkerModel.findIdByPublicId(workerPublicId);
    if (!worker) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    if (!worker.isActive) throw new Error("WORKER_INACTIVE");

    let clientId: number | null = null;
    if (data.clientPublicId) {
      clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(data.clientPublicId);
      if (!clientId) throw new Error("CLIENT_NOT_FOUND");
    }

    let total = 0;
    const preparedItems: Array<{
      productId: number;
      quantity: number;
      unitPrice: number;
      observation?: string | null;
    }> = [];

    for (const item of data.items) {
      const product = await ProductModel.findById(item.productId);
      if (!product) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
      if (!product.isActive) throw new Error("PRODUCT_INACTIVE");

      total += product.price * item.quantity;
      preparedItems.push({
        productId: item.productId,
        quantity: item.quantity,
        unitPrice: product.price,
        observation: item.observation ?? null,
      });
    }

    return OrderModel.create({
      clientId,
      publicId: SecurityUtils.generatePublicId(),
      code: SecurityUtils.generateOrderCode(),
      totalAmount: total,
      status: OrderStatus.CRIADO,
      observation: data.observation ?? null,
      items: preparedItems,
      paymentMethod: data.paymentMethod ?? null,
      actorWorkerId: worker.id,
    });
  }

  static async updateStatusByWorker(
    publicId: string,
    workerPublicId: string,
    newStatus: OrderStatusType,
  ): Promise<OrderDTO> {
    if (!OrderStatus.isValid(newStatus)) throw new Error("INVALID_STATUS");

    const worker = await WorkerModel.findIdByPublicId(workerPublicId);
    if (!worker) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    if (!worker.isActive) throw new Error("WORKER_INACTIVE");

    const order = await OrderModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!order) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");

    if (order.status === newStatus) return order;

    const allowed = ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[order.status] ?? [];
    if (!allowed.includes(newStatus)) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION");
    }

    const updated = await OrderModel.updateStatus(publicId, order.status, newStatus, worker.id);
    if (!updated) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async cancel(
    publicId: string,
    actor: { publicId: string; isWorkerOrAdmin: boolean },
  ): Promise<OrderDTO> {
    const order = await OrderModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!order) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");

    if (!actor.isWorkerOrAdmin) {
      const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(actor.publicId);
      if (!clientId || order.clientId !== clientId) {
        throw new Error("FORBIDDEN");
      }
    }

    if (order.status === OrderStatus.CANCELADO) {
      throw new Error("ORDER_ALREADY_CANCELLED");
    }

    const updated = await OrderModel.cancel(publicId, order.status);
    if (!updated) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<void> {
    const deleted = await OrderModel.deleteByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!deleted) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");
  }
}

import * as z from "zod";
import { OrderStatus, PaymentMethod } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";

export const CreateOrderSchema = z.object({
  clientPublicId: z.string().optional(),
  items: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        productId: z.coerce.number().int().positive(),
        quantity: z.coerce.number().int().positive(),
        observation: z.string().nullable().optional(),
      }),
    )
    .min(1, "Informe ao menos um item para o pedido."),
  paymentMethod: z.enum(PaymentMethod.VALUES, { message: "Método de pagamento inválido." }).optional(),
  observation: z.string().nullable().optional(),
});

export const UpdateOrderStatusSchema = z.object({
  status: z.enum(OrderStatus.VALUES, { message: "Status do pedido inválido." }),
});

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { PaymentService } from "../services/payment.service.js";
import type { SimulatePaymentInput } from "../dtos/payment.dto.js";

export class PaymentController {
  static async simulateCheckout(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const response = await PaymentService.simulateCheckout(
        publicId,
        req.body as SimulatePaymentInput,
      );
      res.status(201).json(response);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

import type * as z from "zod";
import type { OrderDTO } from "../../order/dtos/order.dto.js";
import type { SimulatePaymentSchema } from "../validators/payment.validator.js";

export interface PaymentDTO {
  id: number;
  orderId: number;
  amount: number;
  method: string;
  status: string;
  paidAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface SimulatePaymentResponseDTO {
  data: {
    order: OrderDTO;
    payment: PaymentDTO;
  };
  message: string;
}

export type SimulatePaymentInput = z.infer<typeof SimulatePaymentSchema>;

ARQUIVO [065/252]: Backend/api/payment/models/payment.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 133
========================================================================================
import { withTransaction } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import { OrderStatus } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import { OrderModel } from "../../order/models/order.model.js";
import type { OrderDTO } from "../../order/dtos/order.dto.js";
import type { PaymentDTO } from "../dtos/payment.dto.js";

interface CartItemTxRow {
  cart_id: number;
  product_id: number;
  quantity: number;
  observation: string | null;
  price: string | number;
  is_active: boolean;
}

interface PaymentRow {
  id: number;
  order_id: number;
  amount: string | number;
  method: string;
  status: string;
  paid_at: Date | null;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export class PaymentModel {
  static async checkoutFromCart(input: {
    clientId: number;
    publicId: string;
    code: string;
    paymentMethod: string;
    observation: string | null;
  }): Promise<{ order: OrderDTO; payment: PaymentDTO }> {
    return withTransaction(async (client) => {
      const cartRes = await client.query<{ id: number }>(
        `SELECT id FROM public.carts WHERE client_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
        [input.clientId],
      );

      if (cartRes.rows.length === 0) {
        throw new Error("CART_EMPTY");
      }

      const cartId = cartRes.rows[0].id;

      const itemsRes = await client.query<CartItemTxRow>(
        `SELECT ci.cart_id, ci.product_id, ci.quantity, ci.observation, p.price, p.is_active
         FROM public.cart_items ci
         INNER JOIN public.products p ON p.id = ci.product_id
         WHERE ci.cart_id = $1`,
        [cartId],
      );

      if (itemsRes.rows.length === 0) {
        throw new Error("CART_EMPTY");
      }

      let total = 0;
      for (const item of itemsRes.rows) {
        if (!item.is_active) throw new Error("PRODUCT_INACTIVE");
        total += Number(item.price) * item.quantity;
      }

      const totalFormatted = total.toFixed(2);

      const orderInsert = await client.query<{ id: number }>(
        `INSERT INTO public.orders (public_id, code, client_id, total_amount, status, observation)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::public.order_status, $6)
         RETURNING id`,
        [
          input.publicId,
          input.code,
          input.clientId,
          totalFormatted,
          OrderStatus.CRIADO,
          input.observation,
        ],
      );

      const orderId = orderInsert.rows[0].id;

      for (const item of itemsRes.rows) {
        await client.query(
          `INSERT INTO public.order_items (order_id, product_id, quantity, unit_price, observation)
           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
          [
            orderId,
            item.product_id,
            item.quantity,
            Number(item.price).toFixed(2),
            item.observation,
          ],
        );
      }

      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO public.order_status_history (order_id, previous_status, new_status, changed_by_worker)
         VALUES ($1, $2::public.order_status, $3::public.order_status, NULL)`,
        [orderId, OrderStatus.CRIADO, OrderStatus.CRIADO],
      );

      const paymentInsert = await client.query<PaymentRow>(
        `INSERT INTO public.payments (order_id, amount, method, status, paid_at)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3::public.payment_method, 'confirmado', NOW())
         RETURNING id, order_id, amount, method::text AS method, status::text AS status, paid_at, created_at, updated_at`,
        [orderId, totalFormatted, input.paymentMethod],
      );

      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.cart_items WHERE cart_id = $1`, [cartId]);
      await client.query(`UPDATE public.carts SET updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [cartId]);

      const order = await OrderModel.findByPublicId(input.publicId, client);
      if (!order) throw new Error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND");

      const p = paymentInsert.rows[0];
      return {
        order,
        payment: {
          id: p.id,
          orderId: p.order_id,
          amount: Number(p.amount),
          method: p.method,
          status: p.status,
          paidAt: p.paid_at ? p.paid_at.toISOString() : null,
          createdAt: p.created_at.toISOString(),
          updatedAt: p.updated_at.toISOString(),
        },
      };
    });
  }
}

ARQUIVO [067/252]: Backend/api/payment/services/payment.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 33

import { PaymentMethod } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import { SecurityUtils } from "../../../core/security/security.utils.js";
import { UserModel } from "../../user/models/user.model.js";
import { PaymentModel } from "../models/payment.model.js";
import type { SimulatePaymentInput, SimulatePaymentResponseDTO } from "../dtos/payment.dto.js";

export class PaymentService {
  static async simulateCheckout(
    clientPublicId: string,
    data: SimulatePaymentInput,
  ): Promise<SimulatePaymentResponseDTO> {
    if (!PaymentMethod.isValid(data.paymentMethod)) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_PAYMENT_METHOD");
    }

    const clientId = await UserModel.findInternalIdByPublicId(clientPublicId);
    if (!clientId) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    const { order, payment } = await PaymentModel.checkoutFromCart({
      clientId,
      publicId: SecurityUtils.generatePublicId(),
      code: SecurityUtils.generateOrderCode(),
      paymentMethod: data.paymentMethod,
      observation: data.observation ?? null,
    });

    return {
      data: { order, payment },
      message: "Pagamento simulado com sucesso.",
    };
  }
}

export const SimulatePaymentSchema = z.object({

    message: "Método de pagamento inválido.",

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { ProductService } from "../services/product.service.js";
import type {
  CreateProductInput,
  UpdateProductInput,
  UpdateProductStockInput,
} from "../dtos/product.dto.js";

export class ProductController {
  static async list(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const hasFilters = Boolean(
        req.query.category || req.query.search || req.query.minPrice || req.query.maxPrice,
      );

      if (hasFilters) {
        const products = await ProductService.filter({
          category: typeof req.query.category === "string" ? req.query.category : undefined,
          search: typeof req.query.search === "string" ? req.query.search : undefined,
          minPrice: req.query.minPrice ? Number(req.query.minPrice) : undefined,
          maxPrice: req.query.maxPrice ? Number(req.query.maxPrice) : undefined,
        });
        res.status(200).json({ products, total: products.length, page: 1, limit: products.length || 50 });
        return;
      }

      const page = req.query.page ? Number(req.query.page) : 1;
      const limit = req.query.limit ? Number(req.query.limit) : 50;
      const result = await ProductService.paginate(page, limit);
      res.status(200).json(result);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async listCategories(_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const categories = await ProductService.getCategories();
      res.status(200).json({ data: categories });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async getByPublicId(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const product = await ProductService.getByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: product });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async getStock(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const stock = await ProductService.getStock(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: stock });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async create(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const product = await ProductService.create(req.body as CreateProductInput);
      res.status(201).json({ data: product, message: "Produto criado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async update(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const product = await ProductService.update(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        req.body as UpdateProductInput,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: product, message: "Produto atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async remove(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const result = await ProductService.deleteOrDeactivate(String(req.params.publicId));
      if (result.softDeleted) {
        res.status(200).json({
          data: result.product,
          message: "Produto possui histórico de pedidos e foi desativado.",
        });
        return;
      }
      res.status(204).send();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async uploadImage(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const buffer = req.file?.buffer;
      if (!buffer) {
        res.status(400).json({
          error: { code: "INVALID_REQUEST", message: "Nenhuma imagem enviada." },
        });
        return;
      }
      const product = await ProductService.uploadImage(String(req.params.publicId), buffer);
      res.status(200).json({ data: product, message: "Imagem atualizada com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async activate(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const product = await ProductService.activate(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: product, message: "Produto ativado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async deactivate(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const product = await ProductService.deactivate(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: product, message: "Produto desativado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateStock(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const stock = await ProductService.updateStock(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        req.body as UpdateProductStockInput,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: stock, message: "Estoque atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

import type * as z from "zod";
import type { ProductCategoryType } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import type {
  CreateProductSchema,
  UpdateProductSchema,
  UpdateProductStockSchema,
} from "../validators/product.validator.js";

export interface ProductDTO {
  id: number;
  publicId: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  imgUrl: string | null;
  price: number;
  category: ProductCategoryType;
  isActive: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface ProductStockDTO {
  productId: number;
  quantity: number;
  minQuantity: number;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface PaginatedProductsDTO {
  products: ProductDTO[];
  total: number;
  page: number;
  limit: number;
}

export interface FilterProductsInput {
  category?: string;
  minPrice?: number;
  maxPrice?: number;
  search?: string;
  page?: number;
  limit?: number;
}

export type CreateProductInput = z.infer<typeof CreateProductSchema>;
export type UpdateProductInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateProductSchema>;
export type UpdateProductStockInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateProductStockSchema>;
