ARQUIVO [071/252]: Backend/api/product/models/product.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 243
========================================================================================
import { pool, withTransaction } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import { ProductCategory, type ProductCategoryType } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import type { FilterProductsInput, ProductDTO, ProductStockDTO } from "../dtos/product.dto.js";

interface ProductRow {
  id: number;
  public_id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  category: ProductCategoryType;
  price: string | number;
  img_url: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

interface StockRow {
  product_id: number;
  quantity: number;
  min_quantity: number;
  updated_at: Date;
}

function mapProductRow(row: ProductRow): ProductDTO {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    publicId: row.public_id,
    name: row.name,
    description: row.description,
    imgUrl: row.img_url,
    price: Number(row.price),
    category: row.category,
    isActive: row.is_active,
    createdAt: row.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

const PRODUCT_FIELDS = `
  id, public_id, name, description, category, price, img_url, is_active, created_at, updated_at
`;

export class ProductModel {
  static async findAll(): Promise<ProductDTO[]> {
    const res = await pool.query<ProductRow>(
      `SELECT ${PRODUCT_FIELDS} FROM public.products ORDER BY id ASC`,
    );
    return res.rows.map(mapProductRow);
  }

  static async findPaginated(
    page: number,
    limit: number,
  ): Promise<{ products: ProductDTO[]; total: number }> {
    const offset = (page - 1) * limit;
    const [rowsRes, countRes] = await Promise.all([
      pool.query<ProductRow>(
        `SELECT ${PRODUCT_FIELDS} FROM public.products ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`,
        [limit, offset],
      ),
      pool.query<{ total: string }>(`SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS total FROM public.products`),
    ]);

    return {
      products: rowsRes.rows.map(mapProductRow),
      total: Number(countRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0),
    };
  }

  static async filter(params: FilterProductsInput): Promise<ProductDTO[]> {
    const conditions: string[] = [];
    const values: unknown[] = [];

    if (params.category && ProductCategory.isValid(params.category)) {
      values.push(params.category);
      conditions.push(`category = $${values.length}::public.product_category`);
    }
    if (params.minPrice !== undefined) {
      values.push(params.minPrice);
      conditions.push(`price >= $${values.length}`);
    }
    if (params.maxPrice !== undefined) {
      values.push(params.maxPrice);
      conditions.push(`price <= $${values.length}`);
    }
    if (params.search) {
      values.push(`%${params.search.trim()}%`);
      conditions.push(`name ILIKE $${values.length}`);
    }

    const whereClause = conditions.length > 0 ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}` : "";
    const res = await pool.query<ProductRow>(
      `SELECT ${PRODUCT_FIELDS} FROM public.products ${whereClause} ORDER BY id ASC`,
      values,
    );
    return res.rows.map(mapProductRow);
  }

  static async findById(id: number): Promise<ProductDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<ProductRow>(
      `SELECT ${PRODUCT_FIELDS} FROM public.products WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [id],
    );
    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapProductRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async findByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<ProductDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<ProductRow>(
      `SELECT ${PRODUCT_FIELDS} FROM public.products WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );
    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapProductRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async findCategories(): Promise<ProductCategoryType[]> {
    const res = await pool.query<{ category: string }>(
      `SELECT DISTINCT category::text AS category FROM public.products ORDER BY category ASC`,
    );
    return res.rows
      .map((r) => r.category)
      .filter((c): c is ProductCategoryType => ProductCategory.isValid(c));
  }

  static async create(input: {
    name: string;
    description: string | null;
    imgUrl: string | null;
    price: number;
    category: ProductCategoryType;
  }): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    return withTransaction(async (client) => {
      const inserted = await client.query<ProductRow>(
        `INSERT INTO public.products (name, description, img_url, price, category, is_active)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::public.product_category, TRUE)
         RETURNING ${PRODUCT_FIELDS}`,
        [input.name, input.description, input.imgUrl, input.price, input.category],
      );

      const product = mapProductRow(inserted.rows[0]);
      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO public.stocks (product_id, quantity, min_quantity) VALUES ($1, 0, 5)`,
        [product.id],
      );

      return product;
    });
  }

  static async updateByPublicId(
    publicId: string,
    fields: {
      name?: string;
      description?: string | null;
      imgUrl?: string | null;
      price?: number;
      category?: ProductCategoryType;
      isActive?: boolean;
    },
  ): Promise<ProductDTO | null> {
    const setClauses: string[] = [];
    const values: unknown[] = [];

    const pushField = (column: string, value: unknown, cast?: string) => {
      values.push(value);
      setClauses.push(`${column} = $${values.length}${cast ?? ""}`);
    };

    if (fields.name !== undefined) pushField("name", fields.name);
    if (fields.description !== undefined) pushField("description", fields.description);
    if (fields.imgUrl !== undefined) pushField("img_url", fields.imgUrl);
    if (fields.price !== undefined) pushField("price", fields.price);
    if (fields.category !== undefined) pushField("category", fields.category, "::public.product_category");
    if (fields.isActive !== undefined) pushField("is_active", fields.isActive);

    if (setClauses.length === 0) {
      return this.findByPublicId(publicId);
    }

    values.push(publicId);
    const res = await pool.query<ProductRow>(
      `UPDATE public.products
       SET ${setClauses.join(", ")}
       WHERE public_id = $${values.length}
       RETURNING ${PRODUCT_FIELDS}`,
      values,
    );

    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapProductRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async hasOrderReferences(productId: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await pool.query<{ exists: boolean }>(
      `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.order_items WHERE product_id = $1) AS exists`,
      [productId],
    );
    return res.rows[0]?.exists === true;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await pool.query(`DELETE FROM public.products WHERE public_id = $1`, [publicId]);
    return (res.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
  }

  static async getStock(productId: number): Promise<ProductStockDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<StockRow>(
      `SELECT product_id, quantity, min_quantity, updated_at FROM public.stocks WHERE product_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [productId],
    );
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return null;
    const row = res.rows[0];
    return {
      productId: row.product_id,
      quantity: row.quantity,
      minQuantity: row.min_quantity,
      updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
    };
  }

  static async upsertStock(
    productId: number,
    quantity: number,
    minQuantity?: number,
  ): Promise<ProductStockDTO> {
    const res = await pool.query<StockRow>(
      `INSERT INTO public.stocks (product_id, quantity, min_quantity)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
       ON CONFLICT (product_id) DO UPDATE
       SET quantity = EXCLUDED.quantity,
           min_quantity = COALESCE($4, public.stocks.min_quantity)
       RETURNING product_id, quantity, min_quantity, updated_at`,
      [productId, quantity, minQuantity ?? 0, minQuantity ?? null],
    );
    const row = res.rows[0];
    return {
      productId: row.product_id,
      quantity: row.quantity,
      minQuantity: row.min_quantity,
      updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
    };
  }
}

import { Router } from "express";
import { AuthMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/auth.middleware.js";
import { requireAdmin } from "../../../core/middlewares/permission.middleware.js";
import { avatarUploadMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/upload.middleware.js";
import { validateSchemaMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/validation.middleware.js";
import { ProductController } from "../controllers/product.controller.js";
import {
  CreateProductSchema,
  UpdateProductSchema,
  UpdateProductStockSchema,
} from "../validators/product.validator.js";

export const productRoutes = Router();

productRoutes.use(AuthMiddleware);

productRoutes.get("/", (req, res, next) => void ProductController.list(req, res, next));
productRoutes.get("/categories", (req, res, next) =>
  void ProductController.listCategories(req, res, next),
);
productRoutes.get("/:publicId", (req, res, next) =>
  void ProductController.getByPublicId(req, res, next),
);
productRoutes.get("/:publicId/stock", (req, res, next) =>
  void ProductController.getStock(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.post(
  "/",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(CreateProductSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void ProductController.create(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.put(
  "/:publicId",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateProductSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void ProductController.update(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.delete("/:publicId", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void ProductController.remove(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.post(
  "/:publicId/image",
  requireAdmin(),
  avatarUploadMiddleware,
  (req, res, next) => void ProductController.uploadImage(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.patch("/:publicId/activate", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void ProductController.activate(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.patch("/:publicId/deactivate", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void ProductController.deactivate(req, res, next),
);

productRoutes.patch(
  "/:publicId/stock",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateProductStockSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void ProductController.updateStock(req, res, next),
);

ARQUIVO [073/252]: Backend/api/product/services/product.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 128
========================================================================================
import { imageService } from "../../../core/storage/image.service.js";
import { r2StorageProvider } from "../../../core/storage/bucket.provider.js";
import { ProductModel } from "../models/product.model.js";
import type {
  CreateProductInput,
  FilterProductsInput,
  PaginatedProductsDTO,
  ProductDTO,
  ProductStockDTO,
  UpdateProductInput,
  UpdateProductStockInput,
} from "../dtos/product.dto.js";
import type { ProductCategoryType } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";

export class ProductService {
  static async paginate(page = 1, limit = 50): Promise<PaginatedProductsDTO> {
    const safePage = Math.max(1, page);
    const safeLimit = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, limit));
    const { products, total } = await ProductModel.findPaginated(safePage, safeLimit);
    return { products, total, page: safePage, limit: safeLimit };
  }

  static async filter(params: FilterProductsInput): Promise<ProductDTO[]> {
    return ProductModel.filter(params);
  }

  static async getByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    const product = await ProductModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!product) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return product;
  }

  static async getCategories(): Promise<ProductCategoryType[]> {
    return ProductModel.findCategories();
  }

  static async create(data: CreateProductInput): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    return ProductModel.create({
      name: data.name,
      description: data.description ?? null,
      imgUrl: data.imgUrl ?? null,
      price: data.price,
      category: data.category,
    });
  }

  static async update(publicId: string, data: UpdateProductInput): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    const updated = await ProductModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, {
      name: data.name,
      description: data.description,
      imgUrl: data.imgUrl,
      price: data.price,
      category: data.category,
      isActive: data.isActive,
    });
    if (!updated) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async uploadImage(publicId: string, buffer: Buffer): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    const current = await ProductModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");

    const processed = await imageService.processAvatar(buffer);
    const key = imageService.generateAvatarKey("products", publicId);
    const uploaded = await r2StorageProvider.upload({
      key,
      body: processed.large,
      contentType: processed.contentType,
    });

    if (current.imgUrl) {
      await r2StorageProvider.delete(current.imgUrl).catch(() => undefined);
    }

    const updated = await ProductModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, { imgUrl: uploaded.url });
    if (!updated) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async deleteOrDeactivate(
    publicId: string,
  ): Promise<{ softDeleted: boolean; product?: ProductDTO }> {
    const current = await ProductModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");

    const hasOrders = await ProductModel.hasOrderReferences(current.id);
    if (hasOrders) {
      const deactivated = await ProductModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, { isActive: false });
      if (!deactivated) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
      return { softDeleted: true, product: deactivated };
    }

    if (current.imgUrl) {
      await r2StorageProvider.delete(current.imgUrl).catch(() => undefined);
    }
    await ProductModel.deleteByPublicId(publicId);
    return { softDeleted: false };
  }

  static async activate(publicId: string): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    const updated = await ProductModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, { isActive: true });
    if (!updated) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async deactivate(publicId: string): Promise<ProductDTO> {
    const updated = await ProductModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, { isActive: false });
    if (!updated) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async getStock(publicId: string): Promise<ProductStockDTO | null> {
    const product = await ProductModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!product) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return ProductModel.getStock(product.id);
  }

  static async updateStock(
    publicId: string,
    data: UpdateProductStockInput,
  ): Promise<ProductStockDTO> {
    const product = await ProductModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!product) throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
    return ProductModel.upsertStock(product.id, data.quantity, data.minQuantity);
  }
}

import * as z from "zod";
import { ProductCategory } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";

export const CreateProductSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Nome do produto é obrigatório."),
  description: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  imgUrl: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  price: z.coerce.number().positive("O preço deve ser maior que zero."),
  category: z.enum(ProductCategory.VALUES, { message: "Categoria inválida." }),
});

export const UpdateProductSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Nome do produto não pode ser vazio.").optional(),
  description: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  imgUrl: z.string().nullable().optional(),
  price: z.coerce.number().positive("O preço deve ser maior que zero.").optional(),
  category: z.enum(ProductCategory.VALUES, { message: "Categoria inválida." }).optional(),
  isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
});

export const UpdateProductStockSchema = z.object({
  quantity: z.coerce.number().int().min(0, "Quantidade não pode ser negativa."),
  minQuantity: z.coerce.number().int().min(0, "Quantidade mínima não pode ser negativa.").optional(),
});

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { UserService } from "../services/user.service.js";
import type {
  ToggleUserStatusInput,
  UpdateUserAdminInput,
  UpdateUserSelfInput,
} from "../dtos/user.dto.js";

export class UserController {
  static async getMe(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const user = await UserService.getByPublicId(publicId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: user });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateMe(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const updated = await UserService.updateSelf(
        publicId,
        req.body as UpdateUserSelfInput,
        req.file?.buffer,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: updated, message: "Usuário atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async deleteMe(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      await UserService.deleteByPublicId(publicId);
      res.status(204).send();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async listAll(_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const clients = await UserService.listAll();
      res.status(200).json({ data: clients });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async getById(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const client = await UserService.getByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: client });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateAdmin(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await UserService.updateAdmin(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        req.body as UpdateUserAdminInput,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: updated, message: "Cliente atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async toggleStatus(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const body = (req.body ?? {}) as ToggleUserStatusInput;
      const updated = await UserService.toggleStatus(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        body.isActive,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: updated, message: "Status do cliente atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async remove(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await UserService.deleteByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(204).send();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

import type * as z from "zod";
import type {
  ToggleUserStatusSchema,
  UpdateUserAdminSchema,
  UpdateUserSelfSchema,
} from "../validators/user.validator.js";

export interface UserDTO {
  publicId: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string | null;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  isActive: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export type UpdateUserSelfInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateUserSelfSchema>;
export type UpdateUserAdminInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateUserAdminSchema>;

ARQUIVO [077/252]: Backend/api/user/models/user.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 104
========================================================================================
import { pool } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import type { UserDTO } from "../dtos/user.dto.js";

interface ClientRow {
  id: number;
  public_id: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  phone: string | null;
  avatar_url: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

function mapUserRow(row: ClientRow): UserDTO {
  return {
    publicId: row.public_id,
    email: row.email,
    fullName: row.full_name,
    phone: row.phone,
    avatarUrl: row.avatar_url,
    isActive: row.is_active,
    createdAt: row.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

const USER_SELECT_FIELDS = `
  id, public_id, email, full_name, phone, avatar_url, is_active, created_at, updated_at
`;

export class UserModel {
  static async findAll(): Promise<UserDTO[]> {
    const res = await pool.query<ClientRow>(
      `SELECT ${USER_SELECT_FIELDS} FROM public.clients ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
    );
    return res.rows.map(mapUserRow);
  }

  static async findByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<UserDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<ClientRow>(
      `SELECT ${USER_SELECT_FIELDS} FROM public.clients WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );
    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapUserRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async findInternalIdByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<number | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<{ id: number }>(
      `SELECT id FROM public.clients WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );
    return res.rows[0]?.id ?? null;
  }

  static async updateByPublicId(
    publicId: string,
    fields: {
      fullName?: string;
      email?: string;
      phone?: string | null;
      avatarUrl?: string | null;
      password?: string;
      isActive?: boolean;
    },
  ): Promise<UserDTO | null> {
    const setClauses: string[] = [];
    const values: unknown[] = [];

    const pushField = (column: string, value: unknown) => {
      values.push(value);
      setClauses.push(`${column} = $${values.length}`);
    };

    if (fields.fullName !== undefined) pushField("full_name", fields.fullName);
    if (fields.email !== undefined) pushField("email", fields.email);
    if (fields.phone !== undefined) pushField("phone", fields.phone);
    if (fields.avatarUrl !== undefined) pushField("avatar_url", fields.avatarUrl);
    if (fields.password !== undefined) pushField("password", fields.password);
    if (fields.isActive !== undefined) pushField("is_active", fields.isActive);

    if (setClauses.length === 0) {
      return this.findByPublicId(publicId);
    }

    values.push(publicId);
    const res = await pool.query<ClientRow>(
      `UPDATE public.clients
       SET ${setClauses.join(", ")}
       WHERE public_id = $${values.length}
       RETURNING ${USER_SELECT_FIELDS}`,
      values,
    );

    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapUserRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await pool.query(`DELETE FROM public.clients WHERE public_id = $1`, [publicId]);
    return (res.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
  }
}

import { Router } from "express";
import { AuthMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/auth.middleware.js";
import { requireAdmin } from "../../../core/middlewares/permission.middleware.js";
import { avatarUploadMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/upload.middleware.js";
import { validateSchemaMiddleware } from "../../../core/middlewares/validation.middleware.js";
import { UserController } from "../controllers/user.controller.js";
import {
  ToggleUserStatusSchema,
  UpdateUserAdminSchema,
  UpdateUserSelfSchema,
} from "../validators/user.validator.js";

export const userRoutes = Router();

userRoutes.use(AuthMiddleware);

userRoutes.get("/me", (req, res, next) => void UserController.getMe(req, res, next));

userRoutes.put(
  "/me",
  avatarUploadMiddleware,
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateUserSelfSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void UserController.updateMe(req, res, next),
);

userRoutes.delete("/me", (req, res, next) => void UserController.deleteMe(req, res, next));

userRoutes.get("/", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void UserController.listAll(req, res, next),
);

userRoutes.get("/:publicId", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void UserController.getById(req, res, next),
);

userRoutes.put(
  "/:publicId",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(UpdateUserAdminSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void UserController.updateAdmin(req, res, next),
);

userRoutes.patch(
  "/:publicId/status",
  requireAdmin(),
  validateSchemaMiddleware(ToggleUserStatusSchema),
  (req, res, next) => void UserController.toggleStatus(req, res, next),
);

userRoutes.delete("/:publicId", requireAdmin(), (req, res, next) =>
  void UserController.remove(req, res, next),
);

ARQUIVO [079/252]: Backend/api/user/services/user.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 92

import { SecurityUtils } from "../../../core/security/security.utils.js";
import { imageService } from "../../../core/storage/image.service.js";
import { r2StorageProvider } from "../../../core/storage/bucket.provider.js";
import { UserModel } from "../models/user.model.js";
import type { UpdateUserAdminInput, UpdateUserSelfInput, UserDTO } from "../dtos/user.dto.js";

export class UserService {
  static async listAll(): Promise<UserDTO[]> {
    return UserModel.findAll();
  }

  static async getByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<UserDTO> {
    const user = await UserModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!user) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    return user;
  }

  static async updateSelf(
    publicId: string,
    data: UpdateUserSelfInput,
    avatarBuffer?: Buffer,
  ): Promise<UserDTO> {
    const current = await UserModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");

    if (!avatarBuffer && Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
      throw new Error("UPDATE_REQUIRES_FIELDS");
    }

    let avatarUrl: string | undefined;
    if (avatarBuffer) {
      const processed = await imageService.processAvatar(avatarBuffer);
      const key = imageService.generateAvatarKey("clients", publicId);
      const uploaded = await r2StorageProvider.upload({
        key,
        body: processed.large,
        contentType: processed.contentType,
      });
      if (current.avatarUrl) {
        await r2StorageProvider.delete(current.avatarUrl).catch(() => undefined);
      }
      avatarUrl = uploaded.url;
    }

    const hashedPassword = data.password
      ? await SecurityUtils.hashPassword(data.password)
      : undefined;

    const updated = await UserModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, {
      fullName: data.fullName ?? data.name,
      email: data.email,
      phone: data.phone,
      password: hashedPassword,
      avatarUrl,
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("USER_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async updateAdmin(publicId: string, data: UpdateUserAdminInput): Promise<UserDTO> {
    const current = await UserModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("CLIENT_NOT_FOUND");

    const updated = await UserModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, {
      fullName: data.fullName,
      email: data.email,
      phone: data.phone,
      isActive: data.isActive,
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("CLIENT_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async toggleStatus(publicId: string, explicitStatus?: boolean): Promise<UserDTO> {
    const current = await UserModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("CLIENT_NOT_FOUND");

    const nextStatus = explicitStatus !== undefined ? explicitStatus : !current.isActive;
    const updated = await UserModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, { isActive: nextStatus });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("CLIENT_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<void> {
    const deleted = await UserModel.deleteByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!deleted) throw new Error("CLIENT_NOT_FOUND");
  }
}

import * as z from "zod";

export const UpdateUserSelfSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(2, "Nome deve ter pelo menos 2 caracteres.").optional(),
  fullName: z.string().min(2, "Nome completo deve ter pelo menos 2 caracteres.").optional(),
  email: z.email("E-mail inválido.").optional(),
  phone: z.string().min(1, "Telefone não pode ser vazio.").nullable().optional(),
  password: z.string().min(6, "A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres.").optional(),
});

export const UpdateUserAdminSchema = z
  .object({
    fullName: z.string().min(2, "Nome completo deve ter pelo menos 2 caracteres.").optional(),
    email: z.email("E-mail inválido.").optional(),
    phone: z.string().min(1, "Telefone não pode ser vazio.").nullable().optional(),
    isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
  })
  .refine((data) => Object.keys(data).length > 0, "Informe ao menos um campo para atualização.");

export const ToggleUserStatusSchema = z.object({
  isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
});

ARQUIVO [081/252]: Backend/api/worker/controllers/worker.controller.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 98

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { WorkerService } from "../services/worker.service.js";
import type {
  RegisterWorkerInput,
  ToggleWorkerStatusInput,
  UpdateWorkerAdminInput,
  UpdateWorkerSelfInput,
} from "../dtos/worker.dto.js";

export class WorkerController {
  static async getMe(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const worker = await WorkerService.getByPublicId(publicId);
      res.status(200).json({ data: worker });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateMe(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const publicId = req.user?.publicId;
      if (!publicId) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
      const worker = await WorkerService.updateSelf(
        publicId,
        req.body as UpdateWorkerSelfInput,
        req.file?.buffer,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: worker, message: "Perfil atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async listAll(_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const workers = await WorkerService.listAll();
      res.status(200).json({ data: workers });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async register(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const worker = await WorkerService.create(req.body as RegisterWorkerInput);
      res.status(201).json({ data: worker, message: "Funcionário cadastrado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async getById(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const worker = await WorkerService.getByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(200).json({ data: worker });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async updateAdmin(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const worker = await WorkerService.updateAdmin(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        req.body as UpdateWorkerAdminInput,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: worker, message: "Funcionário atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async toggleStatus(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const body = (req.body ?? {}) as ToggleWorkerStatusInput;
      const worker = await WorkerService.toggleStatus(
        String(req.params.publicId),
        body.isActive,
      );
      res.status(200).json({ data: worker, message: "Status do funcionário atualizado com sucesso." });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  static async remove(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await WorkerService.deleteByPublicId(String(req.params.publicId));
      res.status(204).send();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }
}

ARQUIVO [083/252]: Backend/api/worker/models/worker.model.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 144

import { pool } from "../../../core/database/postgres.client.js";
import type { WorkerRole } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";
import type { WorkerDTO } from "../dtos/worker.dto.js";

interface WorkerRow {
  id: number;
  public_id: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  phone: string | null;
  avatar_url: string | null;
  role: WorkerRole;
  salary: string | number;
  is_admin: boolean;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

function mapWorkerRow(row: WorkerRow): WorkerDTO {
  return {
    publicId: row.public_id,
    email: row.email,
    fullName: row.full_name,
    phone: row.phone,
    avatarUrl: row.avatar_url,
    role: row.role,
    salary: Number(row.salary),
    isAdmin: row.is_admin,
    isActive: row.is_active,
    createdAt: row.created_at.toISOString(),
    updatedAt: row.updated_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

const WORKER_SELECT_FIELDS = `
  id, public_id, email, full_name, phone, avatar_url, role, salary, is_admin, is_active, created_at, updated_at
`;

export class WorkerModel {
  static async findAll(): Promise<WorkerDTO[]> {
    const res = await pool.query<WorkerRow>(
      `SELECT ${WORKER_SELECT_FIELDS} FROM public.workers ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
    );
    return res.rows.map(mapWorkerRow);
  }

  static async findByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<WorkerDTO | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<WorkerRow>(
      `SELECT ${WORKER_SELECT_FIELDS} FROM public.workers WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );
    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapWorkerRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async findIdByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<{ id: number; isActive: boolean } | null> {
    const res = await pool.query<{ id: number; is_active: boolean }>(
      `SELECT id, is_active FROM public.workers WHERE public_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [publicId],
    );
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return null;
    return { id: res.rows[0].id, isActive: res.rows[0].is_active };
  }

  static async create(input: {
    email: string;
    password: string;
    fullName: string;
    phone: string | null;
    role: WorkerRole;
    salary: number;
    isAdmin: boolean;
  }): Promise<WorkerDTO> {
    const res = await pool.query<WorkerRow>(
      `INSERT INTO public.workers (email, password, full_name, phone, role, salary, is_admin, is_active)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::public.worker_roles, $6, $7, TRUE)
       RETURNING ${WORKER_SELECT_FIELDS}`,
      [
        input.email,
        input.password,
        input.fullName,
        input.phone,
        input.role,
        input.salary,
        input.isAdmin,
      ],
    );
    return mapWorkerRow(res.rows[0]);
  }

  static async updateByPublicId(
    publicId: string,
    fields: {
      fullName?: string;
      email?: string;
      phone?: string | null;
      avatarUrl?: string | null;
      password?: string;
      role?: WorkerRole;
      isAdmin?: boolean;
      salary?: number;
      isActive?: boolean;
    },
  ): Promise<WorkerDTO | null> {
    const setClauses: string[] = [];
    const values: unknown[] = [];

    const pushField = (column: string, value: unknown, cast?: string) => {
      values.push(value);
      setClauses.push(`${column} = $${values.length}${cast ?? ""}`);
    };

    if (fields.fullName !== undefined) pushField("full_name", fields.fullName);
    if (fields.email !== undefined) pushField("email", fields.email);
    if (fields.phone !== undefined) pushField("phone", fields.phone);
    if (fields.avatarUrl !== undefined) pushField("avatar_url", fields.avatarUrl);
    if (fields.password !== undefined) pushField("password", fields.password);
    if (fields.role !== undefined) pushField("role", fields.role, "::public.worker_roles");
    if (fields.isAdmin !== undefined) pushField("is_admin", fields.isAdmin);
    if (fields.salary !== undefined) pushField("salary", fields.salary);
    if (fields.isActive !== undefined) pushField("is_active", fields.isActive);

    if (setClauses.length === 0) {
      return this.findByPublicId(publicId);
    }

    values.push(publicId);
    const res = await pool.query<WorkerRow>(
      `UPDATE public.workers
       SET ${setClauses.join(", ")}
       WHERE public_id = $${values.length}
       RETURNING ${WORKER_SELECT_FIELDS}`,
      values,
    );

    return res.rows.length > 0 ? mapWorkerRow(res.rows[0]) : null;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await pool.query(`DELETE FROM public.workers WHERE public_id = $1`, [publicId]);
    return (res.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
  }
}

ARQUIVO [085/252]: Backend/api/worker/services/worker.service.ts
SEÇÃO: 3. Back-end — Módulos da API REST (Backend API) | LINHAS: 123

import { SecurityUtils } from "../../../core/security/security.utils.js";
import { imageService } from "../../../core/storage/image.service.js";
import { r2StorageProvider } from "../../../core/storage/bucket.provider.js";
import { AuthModel } from "../../auth/models/auth.model.js";
import { WorkerModel } from "../models/worker.model.js";
import type {
  RegisterWorkerInput,
  UpdateWorkerAdminInput,
  UpdateWorkerSelfInput,
  WorkerDTO,
} from "../dtos/worker.dto.js";

export class WorkerService {
  static async listAll(): Promise<WorkerDTO[]> {
    return WorkerModel.findAll();
  }

  static async getByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<WorkerDTO> {
    const worker = await WorkerModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!worker) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    return worker;
  }

  static async create(data: RegisterWorkerInput): Promise<WorkerDTO> {
    const existing = await AuthModel.findByEmail(data.email);
    if (existing) throw new Error("EMAIL_ALREADY_IN_USE");

    const hashedPassword = await SecurityUtils.hashPassword(data.password);

    return WorkerModel.create({
      email: data.email,
      password: hashedPassword,
      fullName: data.fullName,
      phone: data.phone ?? null,
      role: data.role,
      salary: data.salary,
      isAdmin: data.role === "gerente",
    });
  }

  static async updateSelf(
    publicId: string,
    data: UpdateWorkerSelfInput,
    avatarBuffer?: Buffer,
  ): Promise<WorkerDTO> {
    const current = await WorkerModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");

    if (!avatarBuffer && Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_REQUEST");
    }

    let avatarUrl: string | undefined;
    if (avatarBuffer) {
      const processed = await imageService.processAvatar(avatarBuffer);
      const key = imageService.generateAvatarKey("workers", publicId);
      const uploaded = await r2StorageProvider.upload({
        key,
        body: processed.large,
        contentType: processed.contentType,
      });
      if (current.avatarUrl) {
        await r2StorageProvider.delete(current.avatarUrl).catch(() => undefined);
      }
      avatarUrl = uploaded.url;
    }

    const hashedPassword = data.password
      ? await SecurityUtils.hashPassword(data.password)
      : undefined;

    const updated = await WorkerModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, {
      fullName: data.fullName ?? data.name,
      email: data.email,
      phone: data.phone,
      password: hashedPassword,
      avatarUrl,
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async updateAdmin(publicId: string, data: UpdateWorkerAdminInput): Promise<WorkerDTO> {
    const current = await WorkerModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");

    const hashedPassword = data.password
      ? await SecurityUtils.hashPassword(data.password)
      : undefined;

    const updated = await WorkerModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, {
      fullName: data.fullName,
      email: data.email,
      phone: data.phone,
      password: hashedPassword,
      role: data.role,
      isAdmin: data.role !== undefined ? data.role === "gerente" : undefined,
      salary: data.salary,
      isActive: data.isActive,
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async toggleStatus(publicId: string, explicitStatus?: boolean): Promise<WorkerDTO> {
    const current = await WorkerModel.findByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!current) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");

    const nextStatus = explicitStatus !== undefined ? explicitStatus : !current.isActive;
    const updated = await WorkerModel.updateByPublicId(publicId, { isActive: nextStatus });

    if (!updated) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
    return updated;
  }

  static async deleteByPublicId(publicId: string): Promise<void> {
    const deleted = await WorkerModel.deleteByPublicId(publicId);
    if (!deleted) throw new Error("WORKER_NOT_FOUND");
  }
}

import * as z from "zod";
import { WorkerRoles } from "../../../core/enums/index.js";

export const RegisterWorkerSchema = z.object({
  fullName: z.string().min(1, "Nome completo é obrigatório."),
  email: z.email("E-mail inválido."),
  password: z.string().min(6, "A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres."),
  phone: z.string().min(1, "Telefone inválido.").optional(),
  role: z.enum(WorkerRoles.VALUES, { message: "Cargo inválido." }),
  salary: z.coerce.number().min(0, "Salário deve ser maior ou igual a zero."),
});

export const UpdateWorkerSelfSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Nome não pode ser vazio.").optional(),
  fullName: z.string().min(1, "Nome completo não pode ser vazio.").optional(),
  email: z.email("E-mail inválido.").optional(),
  phone: z.string().min(1, "Telefone não pode ser vazio.").optional(),
  password: z.string().min(6, "A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres.").optional(),
});

export const UpdateWorkerAdminSchema = z
  .object({
    fullName: z.string().min(1, "Nome completo não pode ser vazio.").optional(),
    email: z.email("E-mail inválido.").optional(),
    phone: z.string().min(1, "Telefone não pode ser vazio.").nullable().optional(),
    password: z.string().min(6, "A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres.").optional(),
    salary: z.coerce.number().min(0, "Salário deve ser maior ou igual a zero.").optional(),
    role: z.enum(WorkerRoles.VALUES, { message: "Cargo inválido." }).optional(),
    isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
  })
  .refine((data) => Object.keys(data).length > 0, "Informe ao menos um campo para atualização.");

export const ToggleWorkerStatusSchema = z.object({
  isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
});

# Multi-stage Dockerfile — Black Amber Coffee API (Clean MVC + PostgreSQL)

# ── Stage 1: Build ──────────────────────────────────────────
FROM node:22-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

# ── Stage 2: Runtime ────────────────────────────────────────
FROM node:22-alpine AS runtime
WORKDIR /app

RUN apk add --no-cache curl

COPY --from=builder /app/dist ./dist
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci --omit=dev

ENV NODE_ENV=production
ENV PORT=3001

EXPOSE ${PORT}

HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=15s --retries=3 \
  CMD curl --fail http://localhost:${PORT}/v1/api/health || exit 1

CMD ["node", "dist/src/server.js"]

ARQUIVO [094/252]: Frontend/packages/utils/helpers/currency.ts
SEÇÃO: 5. Front-end — Pacote Compartilhado de Utilitários e Tipos (packages/utils) | LINHAS: 23

/**
 * Currency formatting helpers — shared across apps.
 */

/**
 * Formats a numeric price value to a display string.
 * @param value  — the numeric price
 * @param locale — BCP-47 locale string (default: "en-US")
 * @param currency — ISO 4217 currency code (default: "USD")
 */
export function formatPrice(
    value: number,
    locale: string = "en-US",
    currency: string = "USD"
): string {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
        style: "currency",
        currency,
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    }).format(value);
}

ARQUIVO [095/252]: Frontend/packages/utils/hooks/useDragScroll.ts
SEÇÃO: 5. Front-end — Pacote Compartilhado de Utilitários e Tipos (packages/utils) | LINHAS: 61
========================================================================================
import { useState, useRef, type MouseEvent } from 'react';

export function useDragScroll<T extends HTMLElement>() {
  const ref = useRef<T>(null);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  
  // Posicionamento X (Horizontal)
  const [startX, setStartX] = useState(0);
  const [scrollLeft, setScrollLeft] = useState(0);

  // Posicionamento Y (Vertical)
  const [startY, setStartY] = useState(0);
  const [scrollTop, setScrollTop] = useState(0);

  const onMouseDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    setIsDragging(true);
    
    // Captura as posições iniciais do mouse e do scroll
    setStartX(e.pageX - ref.current.offsetLeft);
    setScrollLeft(ref.current.scrollLeft);
    
    setStartY(e.pageY - ref.current.offsetTop);
    setScrollTop(ref.current.scrollTop);
  };

  const onMouseLeave = () => {
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const onMouseUp = () => {
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
    if (!isDragging || !ref.current) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    
    // Calcula a distância arrastada no eixo X
    const x = e.pageX - ref.current.offsetLeft;
    const walkX = (x - startX) * 2; // Multiplicador de velocidade horizontal
    ref.current.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - walkX;

    // Calcula a distância arrastada no eixo Y
    const y = e.pageY - ref.current.offsetTop;
    const walkY = (y - startY) * 2; // Multiplicador de velocidade vertical
    ref.current.scrollTop = scrollTop - walkY;
  };

  return {
    ref,
    isDragging,
    events: {
      onMouseDown,
      onMouseLeave,
      onMouseUp,
      onMouseMove,
    }
  };
}

    fullName: string;
    phone: string | null;
    avatarUrl: string | null;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface RegisterResponse {
    data: {
        publicId: string;
        email: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        profile: ProfileResponse;
    };
}

export type UserType = "user" | "worker";

export interface LoginResponse {
    data: {
        accessToken: string;
        refreshToken: string;
        user: {
            publicId: string;
            email: string;
            userType: UserType;
            role?: string | null;
            profile: ProfileResponse;
        };
    };
}

    email: string;
}

export interface CheckPasswordResetInput {
    userTokenId: string;
    code: string;
}

export interface ResetPasswordInput {
    resetToken: string;
    newPassword: string;
    confirmNewPassword: string;
}

export interface RefreshTokenInput {
    refreshToken: string;
}

export interface RefreshTokenResponse {
    data: {
        accessToken: string;
        refreshToken: string;
    };
}

export interface LogoutInput {
    refreshToken: string;
    logoutAllDevices?: boolean;
}

export interface LogoutResponse {
    data: {
        success: boolean;
    };
}

    email: string;
    userTokenId: string | null;
    expiresAt: string;
}

export interface CheckPasswordResetResponse {
    resetToken: string;
}

export interface ResetPasswordResponse {
    accessToken: string;
    refreshToken: string;
    user: {
        publicId: string;
        email: string;
        profile: ProfileResponse;
    };
}

ARQUIVO [102/252]: Frontend/packages/utils/types/inventory.ts
SEÇÃO: 5. Front-end — Pacote Compartilhado de Utilitários e Tipos (packages/utils) | LINHAS: 58
========================================================================================
/**
 * Inventory domain types — shared between admin and client apps.
 */

/** Unit types for inventory amounts */
export const INVENTORY_UNITS = ["kg", "L", "un"] as const;
export type InventoryUnit = (typeof INVENTORY_UNITS)[number];

/** Display labels for each unit */
export const UNIT_LABELS: Record<InventoryUnit, string> = {
    kg: "Kilogram",
    L: "Liter",
    un: "Unit",
};

/** Stock status derived from amount thresholds */
export const STOCK_STATUSES = ["In Stock", "Low Stock", "Out of Stock"] as const;
export type StockStatus = (typeof STOCK_STATUSES)[number];

/** Represents a single item in the inventory */
export interface InventoryItem {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    code: string;
    description?: string;
    amount: number;
    unit: InventoryUnit;
    status: StockStatus;
}

/** Payload used when creating/adding stock */
export interface InventoryAddStockData {
    name: string;
    code: string;
    description?: string;
    amount: number;
    unit: InventoryUnit;
}

/** Payload used when editing an existing inventory item */
export interface InventoryEditData {
    name: string;
    code: string;
    description?: string;
    amount: number;
    unit: InventoryUnit;
}

/**
 * Derives stock status from the current amount.
 * Business rule: 0 = Out of Stock, <= 10 = Low Stock, else In Stock.
 */
export function deriveStockStatus(amount: number): StockStatus {
    if (amount <= 0) return "Out of Stock";
    if (amount <= 10) return "Low Stock";
    return "In Stock";
}

ARQUIVO [108/252]: Frontend/packages/ui-shared/components/CardAnalytics.tsx
SEÇÃO: 6. Front-end — Biblioteca de Componentes Visuais (packages/ui-shared) | LINHAS: 99
========================================================================================
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

type TrendDirection = "up" | "down" | "neutral";

interface CardAnalyticsProps {
    title: string;
    value: string | number;
    delta?: string;
    deltaLabel?: string;
    trend?: TrendDirection;
    icon?: ReactNode;
    iconBgClassName?: string;
    className?: string;
}

const TREND_STYLES: Record<TrendDirection, { text: string; accent: string }> = {
    up: { text: "text-(--Afirmation)", accent: "text-(--Afirmation)" },
    down: { text: "text-(--Negacion)", accent: "text-(--Negacion)" },
    neutral: { text: "text-(--Text-primary-off)", accent: "text-(--Text-primary-off)" },
};

function TrendIcon({ direction }: { direction: TrendDirection }) {
    if (direction === "neutral") {
        return null;
    }

    const rotation = direction === "down" ? "rotate-180" : "";

    return (
        <svg
            className={`w-3 h-3 ${rotation}`}
            viewBox="0 0 12 12"
            fill="none"
            xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
        >
            <path
                d="M6 2L10 8H2L6 2Z"
                fill="currentColor"
            />
        </svg>
    );
}

export function CardAnalytics({
    title,
    value,
    delta,
    deltaLabel,
    trend = "neutral",
    icon,
    iconBgClassName = "bg-(--Primary)",
    className = "",
}: CardAnalyticsProps) {
    const trendStyles = TREND_STYLES[trend];

    return (
        <div
            className={`w-full h-full min-h-29 px-4 py-4 bg-(--Widget-background) rounded-md border border-(--Border) flex flex-col justify-between ${className}`}
        >
            <div className="w-full flex items-start justify-between">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                    <p className="text-(--Text-primary-off) text-xs font-secondary">
                        {title}
                    </p>
                    <h2 className="text-(--Text-gray) text-2xl font-primary font-extrabold">
                        {value}
                    </h2>
                </div>

                {icon && (
                    <div
                        className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center ${iconBgClassName}`}
                    >
                        {icon}
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {(delta || deltaLabel) && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {delta && (
                        <div className={`flex items-center gap-1 ${trendStyles.accent}`}>
                            <TrendIcon direction={trend} />
                            <span className="text-xs font-secondary font-semibold">
                                {delta}
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    )}
                    {deltaLabel && (
                        <span className="text-xs text-(--Text-primary-off) font-secondary">
                            {deltaLabel}
                        </span>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

ARQUIVO [109/252]: Frontend/packages/ui-shared/components/CategoryCarousel.tsx
SEÇÃO: 6. Front-end — Biblioteca de Componentes Visuais (packages/ui-shared) | LINHAS: 55

import { useDragScroll } from '../../utils/hooks/useDragScroll';

interface CategoryCarouselProps {
  categories: string[];
  activeCategory: string;
  onSelectCategory: (category: string) => void;
}

export function CategoryCarousel({ categories, activeCategory, onSelectCategory }: CategoryCarouselProps) {
  const { ref, isDragging, events } = useDragScroll<HTMLDivElement>();

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      {...events}
      style={{
        msOverflowStyle: 'none',
        scrollbarWidth: 'none'
      }}
      className={`
        carousel-container
        flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto px-4 py-2 
        [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden
        ${isDragging ? 'cursor-grabbing' : 'cursor-grab'}
      `}
    >
      <style>{`
        .carousel-container::-webkit-scrollbar {
          display: none !important;
        }
      `}</style>
      {categories.map((category) => {
        const isActive = activeCategory === category;

        return (
          <button
            key={category}
            onClick={() => onSelectCategory(category)}
            className={`
              whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-
              semibold uppercase 
              transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out cursor-pointer
              ${isActive
                ? 'bg-(--Primary) text-(--Text-dark)' // Active state (Yellow)
                : 'bg-(--Button-background) text-(--Text-primary-off) hover:brightness-110' // Inactive state (Dark Gray)
              }
            `}
          >
            {category}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO [110/252]: Frontend/packages/ui-shared/components/ChartAnalytics.tsx
SEÇÃO: 6. Front-end — Biblioteca de Componentes Visuais (packages/ui-shared) | LINHAS: 122
========================================================================================
interface ChartAnalyticsProps {
    title?: string;
    data: number[];
    labels?: string[];
    seriesLabel?: string;
    height?: number;
    accentColor?: string;
}

const DEFAULT_HEIGHT = 220;
const DEFAULT_COLOR = "var(--Primary)";
const GRID_COLUMNS: Record<number, string> = {
    4: "grid-cols-4",
    6: "grid-cols-6",
    12: "grid-cols-12",
};

export function ChartAnalytics({
    title = "Chart",
    data,
    labels,
    seriesLabel = "Series",
    height = DEFAULT_HEIGHT,
    accentColor = DEFAULT_COLOR,
}: ChartAnalyticsProps) {
    const safeData = data.length > 0 ? data : [0];
    const min = Math.min(...safeData);
    const max = Math.max(...safeData);
    const range = max - min || 1;

    const width = 640;
    const chartHeight = 120;
    const paddingX = 10;
    const paddingY = 10;

    const points = safeData
        .map((value, index) => {
            const x =
                paddingX +
                (index / Math.max(safeData.length - 1, 1)) *
                    (width - paddingX * 2);
            const y =
                paddingY +
                (1 - (value - min) / range) *
                    (chartHeight - paddingY * 2);
            return `${x},${y}`;
        })
        .join(" ");

    const labelGridClass = labels ? (GRID_COLUMNS[labels.length] ?? "grid-cols-6") : "";

    return (
        <div className="w-full h-fit p-4 bg-(--Widget-background) rounded-md border border-(--Border) flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-(--Primary-off) text-sm font-primary font-bold">
                    {title}
                </h3>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-(--Text-primary-off) font-secondary">
                    <span
                        className="inline-block w-3 h-3 rounded-full"
                        style={{ background: accentColor }}
                    />
                    {seriesLabel}
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="w-full" style={{ height }}>
                <svg
                    viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${chartHeight}`}
                    className="w-full h-full"
                    preserveAspectRatio="none"
                >
                    <line x1="0" y1="120" x2="640" y2="120" stroke="var(--Border)" />
                    <line x1="0" y1="80" x2="640" y2="80" stroke="var(--Border)" />
                    <line x1="0" y1="40" x2="640" y2="40" stroke="var(--Border)" />

                    <polyline
                        points={points}
                        fill="none"
                        stroke={accentColor}
                        strokeWidth="2"
                    />

                    {safeData.map((value, index) => {
                        const x =
                            paddingX +
                            (index / Math.max(safeData.length - 1, 1)) *
                                (width - paddingX * 2);
                        const y =
                            paddingY +
                            (1 - (value - min) / range) *
                                (chartHeight - paddingY * 2);
                        return (
                            <circle
                                key={`point-${index}`}
                                cx={x}
                                cy={y}
                                r="3"
                                fill="var(--Primary)"
                                stroke="var(--Page-background)"
                                strokeWidth="1"
                            />
                        );
                    })}
                </svg>
            </div>

            {labels && labels.length > 0 && (
                <div
                    className={`grid ${labelGridClass} gap-2 text-[10px] text-(--Text-primary-off) font-secondary`}
                >
                    {labels.map((label, index) => (
                        <span key={`${label}-${index}`} className="text-center">
                            {label}
                        </span>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

ARQUIVO [112/252]: Frontend/packages/ui-shared/components/ConfirmDialog.tsx
SEÇÃO: 6. Front-end — Biblioteca de Componentes Visuais (packages/ui-shared) | LINHAS: 102
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

interface ConfirmDialogProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    title: string;
    description?: string;
    confirmLabel?: string;
    cancelLabel?: string;
    danger?: boolean;
    onConfirm: () => void;
    onCancel: () => void;
}

export function ConfirmDialog({
    isOpen,
    title,
    description,
    confirmLabel = "Confirm",
    cancelLabel = "Cancel",
    danger = false,
    onConfirm,
    onCancel,
}: ConfirmDialogProps) {
    const confirmButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) return;

        confirmButtonRef.current?.focus();

        function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
            if (event.key === "Escape") {
                onCancel();
            }

            if (event.key === "Enter") {
                const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
                const tagName = target?.tagName;
                const isTypingTarget =
                    tagName === "INPUT" ||
                    tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
                    tagName === "SELECT" ||
                    target?.isContentEditable;

                if (!isTypingTarget) {
                    onConfirm();
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    }, [isOpen, onCancel, onConfirm]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center">
            <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Close dialog"
                className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60"
                onClick={onCancel}
            />

            <div className="relative w-90 max-w-[90vw] rounded-sm border border-(--Border) bg-(--Widget-background) p-5 shadow-lg">
                <h3 className="text-(--Text-gray) text-base font-primary font-extrabold">
                    {title}
                </h3>
                {description ? (
                    <p className="mt-2 text-(--Text-primary-off) text-sm font-secondary">
                        {description}
                    </p>
                ) : null}

                <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onCancel}
                        className="px-4 py-2 rounded-sm border border-(--Border) text-(--Text-primary-off) text-xs font-secondary font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:border-(--Primary) hover:text-(--Primary) transition-all duration-200"
                    >
                        {cancelLabel}
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onConfirm}
                        ref={confirmButtonRef}
                        className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-sm text-xs font-secondary font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider border transition-all duration-200
                            ${danger
                                ? "border-(--Negacion)/60 text-(--Negacion) hover:border-(--Negacion)"
                                : "border-(--Border2) text-(--Text-dark) bg-(--Primary) hover:bg-(--Primary-selected)"
                            }
                        `}
                    >
                        {confirmLabel}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

ARQUIVO [113/252]: Frontend/packages/ui-shared/components/FilterButton.tsx
SEÇÃO: 6. Front-end — Biblioteca de Componentes Visuais (packages/ui-shared) | LINHAS: 106

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Check, SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";

export interface FilterOption {
    label: string;
    action: () => void;
    active?: boolean;
}

interface FilterButtonProps {
    options: FilterOption[];
    title?: string;
    buttonLabel?: string;
    align?: "left" | "right";
    closeOnSelect?: boolean;
    emptyLabel?: string;
    disabled?: boolean;
}

export function FilterButton({
    options,
    title = "Filters",
    buttonLabel,
    align = "right",
    closeOnSelect = true,
    emptyLabel = "No filters",
    disabled = false,
}: FilterButtonProps) {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) return;

        function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
            if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsOpen(false);
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    }, [isOpen]);

    return (
        <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => !disabled && setIsOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer
                    ${disabled
                        ? "text-(--Text-primary-off)/60 bg-(--Button-background)/50 cursor-not-allowed"
                        : "text-(--Text-primary-off) hover:text-(--Primary) hover:bg-(--Button-background)"
                    }
                `}
                aria-label="Filter"
                aria-expanded={isOpen}
                disabled={disabled}
            >
                <SlidersHorizontal size={18} />
                {buttonLabel ? <span className="text-sm font-secondary">{buttonLabel}</span> : null}
            </button>

            {isOpen && (
                <div
                    className={`absolute ${align === "right" ? "right-0" : "left-0"} top-full mt-2 w-56 bg-(--Page-background) border border-(--Border) rounded-md shadow-lg z-50 overflow-hidden animate-[slideIn_0.15s_ease-out]`}
                >
                    {title ? (
                        <div className="px-4 py-3 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-(--Text-primary-off) border-b border-(--Border)">
                            {title}
                        </div>
                    ) : null}

                    <div className="py-1">
                        {options.length === 0 ? (
                            <div className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-(--Text-primary-off)">{emptyLabel}</div>
                        ) : (
                            options.map((option) => (
                                <button
                                    key={option.label}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => {
                                        option.action();
                                        if (closeOnSelect) {
                                            setIsOpen(false);
                                        }
                                    }}
                                    className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-secondary transition-colors cursor-pointer
                                        ${option.active
                                            ? "text-(--Primary) bg-(--Select-background)"
                                            : "text-(--Text-gray) hover:bg-(--Select-background)"
                                        }
                                    `}
                                >
                                    <span className="truncate">{option.label}</span>
                                    {option.active ? <Check size={16} /> : null}
                                </button>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

import {
    Bell
}
    from "lucide-react";

import {useState, useRef, useEffect} from "react";


export interface NotificationProps {
    id: number;
    message: string;
    time: string;
    action: () => void;
}


interface NotificationBellProps {
    notifications?: NotificationProps[];
}



export function NotificationBell({ notifications = [] }: NotificationBellProps) {

    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const bellRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);


    useEffect(() => {
        function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
            if (isOpen && bellRef.current && !bellRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
                setIsOpen(false);
            }
        }
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);

        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    return (
        <div className="relative" ref={bellRef}>
            <button
                onClick={() => setIsOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                className="relative p-1.5 rounded-md text-(--Text-primary-off) hover:text-(--Primary) hover:bg-(--Button-background) transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
                aria-label="Notifications"
            >
                <Bell size={18} className="cursor-pointer text-(--Primary-off) hover:text-(--Primary-selected)" />
                
                {notifications.length > 0 && (
                    <span className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-(--Primary) ring-2 ring-(--Page-background)" />
                )}
            </button>

            {isOpen && (
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-1 w-64 bg-(--Page-background) border border-(--Border) rounded-sm shadow-lg z-50 overflow-hidden animate-[slideIn_0.15s_ease-out]">
                    {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-(--Text-primary-off)">
                            No new notifications
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        notifications.map((notification) => (
                            <div
                                key={notification.id}
                                onClick={() => { notification.action(); setIsOpen(false); }}
                                className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-(--Border) cursor-pointer hover:bg-(--Select-background) transition-colors"
                            >
                                <div className="text-sm text-(--Text-gray)">{notification.message}</div>
                                <div className="text-xs text-(--Text-primary-off)">{notification.time}</div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

ARQUIVO [117/252]: Frontend/packages/ui-shared/components/OptionElipisses.tsx
